### Page 3

Company profile
Yageo is the global largest R-chip and tantalum capacitor and 3rd largest MLCC and inductor supplier (in terms of capacity). It has high market
share in global passive components industry.
Valuation Methodology
Our target price for Yageo is TWD1,350, which is based on 30x 2028F EPS of TWD45.0. Our 30x P/E multiple is at the higher end of 8-40x P/E
range since 2018. The benchmark index is TAIEX.
Risks that may impede the achievement of the target price

Key downside risks: 1) longer than expected inventory correction resulting in a low UTR, which would pressure ASPs; 2) failure to pass through high
raw material costs from the high inflation in 2021; 3) intensifying competition from Greater China vendors; and 4) Higher OPEX than expected by
new sensor business
ESG
Yageo corporation declares the metal conflict-free on products supplied to consumers. Yageo take due diligence with supply chain to assure the

metals are not derived from or sourced from mines in conflict areas. Yageo establish and implement enviromental management of energy saving
and waste minimization, strengthen resources recycling and reuse, reduce and disuse environment-prohibited material.

Definition of Nomura Group's equity research rating system and sectors
The rating system is a relative system, indicating expected performance against a specific benchmark identified for each individual stock, 
subject to limited management discretion. An analyst’s target price is an assessment of the current intrinsic fair value of the stock based on an 
appropriate valuation methodology determined by the analyst. Valuation methodologies include, but are not limited to, discounted cash flow 
analysis, expected return on equity and multiple analysis. Analysts may also indicate expected absolute upside/downside relative to the stated 
target price, defined as (target price - current price)/current price.

SECTORS
A 'Bullish' stance, indicates that the analyst expects the sector to outperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Neutral' stance, 
indicates that the analyst expects the sector to perform in line with the Benchmark during the next 12 months. A 'Bearish' stance, indicates that 
the analyst expects the sector to underperform the Benchmark during the next 12 months. Sectors that are labelled as 'Not rated' or shown as 
'N/A' are not assigned ratings. Benchmarks are as follows: United States: S&P 500; Europe: Dow Jones STOXX 600; Global Emerging 
Markets (ex-Asia): MSCI Emerging Markets ex-Asia. Japan/Asia ex-Japan: Sector ratings are not assigned.

Target Price
A Target Price, if discussed, indicates the analyst’s forecast for the share price with a 12-month time horizon, reflecting in part the analyst's 
estimates for the company's earnings. The achievement of any target price may be impeded by general market and macroeconomic trends, and 
by other risks related to the company or the market, and may not occur if the company's earnings differ from estimates.

### Page 1

ab 1 July 2026
Global Research
ASE Industrial
Higher capex to power larger advanced
packaging opportunity
Higher capex suggests stronger advanced packaging sales
ASE has rallied 171% YTD, outperforming the 59% increase in Taiex, and we expect its
strength to continue. We believe ASE is in the early stage of a multi-year fundamental
transformation due to Cloud AI and the fast growing advanced packaging opportunity,
and that ASE's industry position is strengthening. At its recent AGM, management

flagged upside potential for its already elevated 2026 capex of US$8.5bn vs. TSMC's
back-end capex of cUS$8.3bn in 2026. We raise our ASE 2026/27 capex forecasts to US
$9.0bn/10.0bn from US$8.5bn/9.0bn.
LEAP sales to rise to US$3.6bn/6.7bn in 2026/27E
Our recent industry analysis indicates that ASE has been further accelerating full process
CoWoS expansion. We forecast ASE's CoWoS capacity to increase from c 20kwpm at
end-2026 to c50kwpm at end-2027 vs. our prior estimate of 35-40kwpm. We think the

significant expansion is to support stronger demand for AMD's new Venice server CPUs
and possible upside for AI accelerators. We forecast ASE's full process CoWoS sales to
rise from US$ 0.3bn in 2026 to US$ 2.0bn in 2027 with upside risk. The outlook for
TSMC's on-substrate outsourcing, probing and final test has been strengthening as well.
For final test, we expect ASE to grow sales in 2027E via Amazon’s Trainium 3 and
MediaTek’s TPU v8t, both of which are set to ramp meaningfully next year. We forecast

ASE's LEAP platform sales to rise from US$3.6bn in 2026 to US$6.7bn in 2027 vs. our
prior estimate of US$5.8bn (see   Figure 1A S E ' s  l e a d i n g - e d g e  a d v a n c e d  p a c k a g i n g  b u s i n e s s         , for our detailed LEAP sales forecasts). We raise
our IC ATM GM to 27.9%/32.8%/33.7% in 2026/27/28E.
Healthy outlook for mainstream packaging & testing
The traditional back-end outlook appears better than expected in coming quarters. We

expect mature foundry utilisation to further improve in H226-2027E with stable
consumer restocking, growing demand for servers, and an improving auto and industrial
cycle. This should support ASE's mainstream semis sales growth to the guided 13-14%
in 2026 and sustain growth in 2027E.
Valuation:  raise price target to NT$835.00 from NT$660.00; reiterate Buy
We fine tune 2026E EPS and raise 2027/28E EPS  by 11%/10% to factor in our more
optimistic LEAP outlook. Our new PT is based on 25x 2027-28E PE vs. 25x 2027E PE

previously, justified by 26% long-term earnings CAGR forecast. Key catalyst could be its
upcoming earnings conference in late July, as we anticipate another solid set of earnings
and guidance, and a potential capex upgrade.

### Page 2

ASE Industrial 1 July 2026 ab 2
ASE Industrial UBS ResearchASE Industrial
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Can ASE’s IC ATM GM continue to improve and be in line with its structural GM target?
Likely.  ASE's IC ATM GM reached 26.0% in Q126, and  management expects a high 20-30% range by
H226. We are optimistic about ASE's GM outlook in the next two to three years  with strong

expansion of LEAP , increasing turnkey testing and a rebound in non-AI demand. We forecast ASE's IC
ATM GM to improve to 27.9% in 2026 and 32.8% in 2027 vs. 22.6% in 2023-25.
Q: Could ASE benefit from  structural growth in advanced packaging and testing?
Yes. We expect ASE to grow LEAP and testing segment sales  to US$3.6bn in 2026E and US$6.7bn in
2027E. We are positive about ASE's opportunity as: 1) it is the largest back-end supplier globally and

has the largest scale to invest in R&D and capex;  2) its close collaboration with TSMC; and 3) upside
potential in testing via the full turnkey service.
We are constructive about ASE's fundamental business transformation due to the LEAP platform and
testing, and we expect meaningful upside for profitability and earnings for 2026E and beyond. We
think ASE’s strong earnings beat in Q1 was further evidence that its investment is starting to pay off

and we expect further upside. We believe  GM is on track to continue to strengthen based on the
rising contribution from LEAP and testing, ASE’s expanding scale and value add, and tighter
traditional packaging supply/demand. ASE’s ramp of full-process CoWoS could accelerate, given its
improving technology maturity and customer diversification along with TSMC being more focused
on higher-end solutions.
In April ASE raised its total capex target to US$8.5bn for 2026, a significant step up from US$5.5bn in

2025. At ASE's AGM in June 2026 , the CEO flagged the possibility of a further increase in capex. The
increased investment is for 2027-28 and also for longer term plans, which could include developing
new manufacturing sites as well as expanding existing sites. This reflects the robust future demand
profile for Cloud AI in the upcoming years.
ASE has rallied 171% YTD, outperforming the 59% increase in Taiex. We believe the stock has room
to continue to re-rate, driven by the increasing advanced packaging TAM and ASE’s improving

position in full-process CoWoS, probing and final test.

Company Description
ASE Industrial is the holding company that controls ASE and Siliconware, and is the world's
largest and assembly and testing service providers in 2024 in terms of revenue. In addition to
semiconductor back-end services, ASE provides electronic manufacturing services (EMS).

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on a PE multiple relative to ASE and SPIL's previous historical
valuation ranges.
We believe ASE Industrial faces a variety of upside and downside risks, including rapidly-
changing technology, intense competition, high capital investment and cyclical demand.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 1

ab 1 July 2026
Global Research
UBS Global I/O Semiconductors
Cloud AI: TSMC and ASE driving faster CoWoS
expansion, unlocking larger TAM
CoWoS capacity upside in 2026-27 across TSMC and ASE
We view TSMC and broader industry CoWoS expansion as a leading indicator of cloud
AI demand over the next 2-3 years. Capacity expansion over the next 12-18 months is
progressing faster than we expected a month ago, suggesting stronger underlying cloud
AI demand. TSMC's CoWoS capacity expansion is re-accelerating and we now expect

capacity to reach 130k/180k wpm by end-2026/27 vs our prior estimates of 120k / 150k
wpm. We believe this reflects a much larger advanced packaging TAM over the next
several years, with a meaningful portion likely to remain on CoWoS even as Intel EMIB-T
and TSMC CoPoS enter mass production in 2028. ASE has also been accelerating its full-
process capacity expansion for 2027. We now forecast its full-process CoWoS capacity
to more than double from 20kwpm at end-2026 E to ~50kwpm end-2027E vs prior

estimate of 40kwpm. (See  our ASE note). Overall, we estimate industry CoWoS capacity
may rise from 160kwpm at end-2026 to 250kwpm by end-2027.
Robust server CPU demand; Nvidia Rubin ramp to accelerate in H226
On the demand side, we believe server CPU continues to show upside into 2027. ASE's
faster CoWoS expansion should be underpinned by higher AMD Venice volumes, which
we estimate at 4mn units in 2027, up from 1.3mn in 2026. AMD's CoWoS demand

could grow 232% in 2027E with a strong ramp of Venice server CPU and MI450/455.
We also lift Nvidia's Vera CPU units to 5.5mn units in 2027 from 1.6mn in 2026,
implying 57% growth in Nvidia's CoWoS demand in 2027. Rubin GPU ramp was slower
in Q226 due to redesigns, but we anticipate a steep pick up in H226 to reach 2.1mn for
2026. We forecast Google TPU chipset units to rise from  4.1mn in 2026 to 9.0mn in
2027, with MediaTek expected to support 4mn units of TPU v8t capacity. Amazon

Trainium 3 demand is growing and we lift 2026/27E unit estimates to 1.8mn/2.8mn
(from 1.7m/2.3m). See our CoWoS supply vs demand analysis in Figure 2T o t a l  C o W o S  i n t e r p o s e r  w a f e r  d e m a n d-Figure 12C o W o S  d e m a n d  f r o m   m a j o r  c u s t o m e r s.
More diversified supply ahead, but TSMC should remain the largest player
As previously noted , we anticipate advanced packaging supply to become more
diversified from 2027-28 onward. We see increasing opportunities for ASE and Amkor in

H226/2027, mainly driven by server CPUs. Intel's EMIB-T is gaining traction, but we
believe Intel may face resource and capacity constraints in 2028, and will likely prioritise
internal products and Google/MediaTek's TPU v9 initially. Overall, we think TSMC's
advanced packaging sales should sustain solid ~50% growth over the next five years on
its leading market share, rising content through 3D stacking, and tech upgrades such as
CoPoS and co-packaged optics.

Stock recommendations; ASE/GPTC price target raised to NT$835/$5,000
Along the semiconductor supply chain for cloud AI, our top picks are TSMC (the
industry's leading cloud AI foundry), MediaTek (design services for Google TPU), and
ASE (advanced packaging & testing). We estimate backend, including advanced
packaging, to reach ~12%/15% of TSMC sales in 2026/27E, and our recent Q226
earnings preview raised our sales and EPS estimates on stronger cloud AI demand.

MediaTek is a Buy and a Key Call. We lift our ASE price target to NT$835 from NT$660.
Critical equipment suppliers for advanced packaging & testing could also benefit from
the cloud AI ramp-up, and we like GPTC,  Chroma, Hon Precision, and ASMPT. We raise
our GPTC price target to NT$5,000 (from NT$4,000) based on 33x 2027-28E PE (vs 33x
2027E PE previously). See  page 6. KYEC remains well positioned in the final test space.
We like Aspeed for its strong BMC outlook, GUC for its robust Google CPU upside

UBS Global I/O Semiconductors UBS ResearchGrand Process Technology
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Can advanced packaging sustain strong growth from cloud AI and increasing chiplet
design into N2?
Yes. Advanced packaging has gained importance amid greater system integration and rising
performance demands. We expect robust CoWoS capacity expansion to continue in 2026-27, while

faster expansion by OSATs such as ASE should benefit GPTC, given its higher share in OSATs. We
expect TSMC to ramp up CoWoS capacity from 70kwpm at end-2025 to 130kwpm/180kwpm by
end-2026/27. We think OSAT's CoWoS capacity could reach 70kwpm by end-2027. We forecast
GPTC’s tool shipments to grow 22%/50% in 2026/2027.
Q: Will competitors cause GM headwinds?
Unlikely. We believe GPTC is well positioned versus competitors, given its strong track record in

developing new technologies and its ability to maintain solid GM. Over the past 10+ years, despite
competition from Scientech in InFO (integrated fan-out) and CoWoS, GPTC has sustained GM above
40% through technology leadership and continuous product roll-outs. Now, with advanced
packaging proliferating amid faster technology upgrades and more design types, we expect GPTC to
benefit over the next few years, supported by its strong industry position and leading market share in

SoIC, panel-level packaging and CPO, among others. GPTC’s GM has come under slight pressure in
2025-26 as it  outsources some module assembly to meet strong demand. However, we expect its GM
to gradually recover toward the mid-40% range by 2027-28.
GPTC is a Taiwanese semiconductor equipment supplier specialising in wet processing tools for
advanced packaging. For 2026, we expect TSMC to contribute around 40-50% of its sales, with ASE
and SPIL contributing around 25-30% combined. We are increasingly optimistic about the

accelerated development of advanced packaging over the next three to four years, and believe GPTC
could gain share given its superior technology capabilities. We raise our earnings estimates for 2026
and beyond to reflect a stronger outlook for CoWoS capacity expansion in 2027, as well as additional
upside from CoPoS, SoIC and CPO in 2028. We forecast GPTC’s tool shipments to increase
substantially  to ~330 units in 2027, up from 220 in 2026. We reiterate our Buy rating and raise our

price target to NT$5,000 from NT$4,000, based on 33x 2027-28E PE (vs 33x 2027E PE previously).
We believe a 33x PE multiple is justified by 36% long-term earnings CAGR.
In recent months, we have observed increasing  signs of strengthening cloud AI demand, with several
large US hyperscalers further raising their 2027 cloud capex plans, alongside TSMC accelerating N3/
N2 capacity expansion and increasing HBM-related capex.
YTD, GPTC's stock has rallied 133%, reflecting an improving cloud AI outlook for 2026 and beyond.

However, we believe the significant opportunities in CoWoS, SoIC, panel-level packaging, HBM and
CPO are still not fully priced in.

Founded in 1993 and headquartered in Hsinchu, Taiwan, Grand Process Technology Corp. (GPTC) is
an industry leader that specializes in semiconductor wet processing equipment and chemicals.
GPTC’s expertise in the wet processing equipment field is recognized by major foundries and
backend semiconductor companies. Its main customers include TSMC, ASE/SPIL and Chinese OSATs,

with around 70% of sales concentrated in Taiwan.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Tech investing involves risk. It is difficult for the investment community, UBS included, to
project the financial results of tech companies, as their operating models are highly volatile
and unpredictable, and they compete in a highly dynamic marketplace. In addition to their
low predictability, valuing tech stocks can be challenging because neither traditional nor non-

traditional valuation measures have provided much insight into how tech stocks trade.
We value GPTC based on PE methodology.
Downside risks include: 1) slower-than-expected CoWoS capacity expansion; 2) competitors
achieving technological advancements; 3) geopolitical uncertainty; and 4) worse-than-
expected AI growth.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Industry View Attractive
Global Technology
NAND Industry Outlook:
Diverging Trends
AI continues to create shortages in the NAND industry into 2027,
while supply and demand dynamics depend on supply discipline
moving into 2028 and beyond. We still like suppliers for L TA

downside protection and enhanced shareholder returns, and are
starting to see consumer pricing plateau.
What’s Changed
Shenzhen Longsys Electronics Co Ltd (301308.SZ) From To
Price Target Rmb300.00 Rmb673.00
Silicon Motion (SIMO.O)
Price Target US$155.00 US$400.00
Phison Electronics Corp (8299.TWO)
Price Target NT$2,248.00 NT$2,588.00
What's changed? We update our global NAND supply and demand forecast for
2026-27 and conducted a scenario test on greenfield expansion vs. AI demand

growth for 2028. Our calculation shows significant shortages should persist into
2027. Moving into 2028, node migration and greenfield expansion may  ease some
supply tightness (-5% shortage if 60% YoY AI NAND growth with current base case
capacity expansion), while in a bear case scenario where both China restrictions  and
supply discipline ease, there is potential risk of oversupply.
Bifurcation – AI vs. consumer:
We see overall server demand remaining strong as

L TAs have provided downside protections on  pricing. On the consumer side,
inventory levels have increased at module makers and distributors, and smartphone
and PC customers are facing increasing pressure on the volume vs. margin trade-off.
This is not new to the market, but we have begun to see actual order cuts after
2Q26 price hikes, indicating pricing  for consumer products might hit a ceiling very
soon while volume remains muted as suppliers shift capacity to AI.

Stock implications: We remain bullish on the memory cycle and continue to like
supplier names with L TA supporting valuation expansion and shareholder returns.
Tactically, we prefer DRAM over NAND due to better L TA terms, higher demand
visibility, supply discipline capped by EUV and a potential HBM4E capacity squeeze.
In NAND, we prefer suppliers over module makers, reflecting margin durability.
Global perspective: In DRAM, Samsung Electronics
is Asia Tech team's Top Pick for

its market leadership and potentially stronger shareholder returns. In NAND, Kioxia
is our Japan Semi team’s Top Pick for solid FCF generation and shareholder return
potential while Macronix is our GC Semi team’s Top Pick. We remain OW on
Micron, SK hynix and SanDisk amid a durable memory cycle, as well as Fadu, a key
eSSD controller supplier to SanDisk. We raise our SIMO PT to reflect the eSSD
opportunity. We remain EW on Longsys and Phison on concerns around muted

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

### Page 2

M
Global Idea
2
Global Perspectives
Sandisk/Micron
Joseph Moore
We remain OW on Sandisk and MU, as supply demand outcomes continue to look
compelling for both NAND and DRAM. With AI now the principal driver of memory bits
and little to no inventory across the supply, the biggest risk to memory stocks is  a broader
AI capex slowdown, which we do not expect to peak in the near term, supporting a path to
higher earnings power well beyond 2027 (when consensus currently assumes pricing will

begin to normalize).  We expect continued upward revisions to estimates, and at  <10x P/E
for both SNDK and MU multiples should still have room to benefit from extended
visibility.  L TAs are part of what's enabling that higher level of visibility, and are
symptomatic of a market that could should stay tight for another 2-3 years. Meanwhile,
MU and Sandisk should continue to be attractive capital return stories, as we expect both
to pursue meaningful buybacks in 2027.

KIOXIA
Kazuo Yoshikawa
We maintain KIOXIA as our Top Pick within our Japan semiconductor coverage, reflecting
solid FCF generation and shareholder return potential.
KIOXIA appears well positioned to capture AI-driven storage demand with a broad SSD
lineup. The CM Series addresses high-throughput, low-latency KV cache needs for
inference AI, while the GP Series targets Super High IOPS applications that can
complement HBM. The LC Series supports large-scale AI storage demand with 245TB QLC

SSDs, with mass-production shipments already underway.
On capital allocation, KIOXIA plans to use FCF for both growth investments and
shareholder returns. We see scope for stronger returns, supported by expected annual
FCF generation of ¥4.0-5.0trn in FY3/27-3/28 even under our conservative ASP
assumptions, and management’s indication that a substantial portion of excess
accumulated FCF could potentially be returned to shareholders.
KIOXIA is also progressing L TA discussions, mainly with data center and enterprise

customers. L TAs are expected to cover more than 50% of CY27 shipments, while CY28
coverage is likely to remain around 50% to preserve flexibility.
Samsung Electronics/SK hynix
Shawn Kim
We maintain OW ratings for both Samsung Electronics (Top Pick) and SK hynix as key
beneficiaries to AI compute and agentic AI trends (see Agentic AI – The Surge Begins). We
estimate more than 20-30% DRAM price hikes in 3Q26, enough to keep the YoY rate of
change accelerating. Pricing power is translating into earnings revisions that in turn

support P/E stability, at 5.2x 2027e earnings.
However, we are mindful of the rate of change that still matters for memory stocks. With
YoY pricing likely to plateau in 4Q26 vs. supply discipline visibility in 2028, we believe

### Page 3

M
Global Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 3
stocks might lack near-term cyclical catalysts until supply and demand dynamics become
more clear into 2028 and beyond. That said, multiples could continue to re-rate on the
back of favorable L TAs.
Longsys
Duan Liu
We have significantly raised our estimates for Longsys on better-than-expected pricing
trends and high conviction levels on 2027 shortages. We expect Longsys's long-term
margin profile to improve due to its better product mix. Its TCM business model will likely

bring a more stable margin profile over the long term (25-35%) as inventory pressure is
shifted to customers, and the company's in-house controller development could further
increase the value add on firmware and customized services. We are slightly concerned
about muted volume growth due to suppliers' shifting capacity to CSP customers, which
should cap near-term volume growth, but this should be well understood by the market
and already in the price.

Phison
Charlie Chan
2Q26 preliminary results were a strong beat vs. estimates, driven by more resilient pricing
hike intervals and shipments than expected. 3Q26 is expected to be the peak quarter of
the year, supported by additional supply support from Kioxia’s dummy die, which could
drive around 20% QoQ revenue growth and a better-than-feared flat QoQ margin.
However, this strength appears cyclical rather than sustainable; gross margin expansion is

still expected to peak around 2Q26–3Q26 as low-cost NAND inventory is depleted and
higher-cost supply begins to flow through under FIFO accounting. By 4Q26, emerging
weakness in consumer tech and a more moderate pricing environment are expected to
drive revenue down around 20% QoQ, with margins normalizing to roughly 50%.
Although the better 2026 pricing environment supports stronger-than-expected
profitability into 2027, this upside is partially offset by continued R&D investment and

broader structural risks, including constrained NAND supply, weakening consumer SSD
demand, limited raw NAND supplier support, modest eSSD revenue contribution of only
around 10–20% for most Asia-based SSD vendors and hyperscalers’ tendency to purchase
directly from NAND fab owners or negotiate long-term supply agreements, which may
limit the long-term addressable market for module vendors such as Phison.
SIMO (PT increases to US$400 from US$155)

Tiffany Yeh
For FY2026, the company expects record revenue, sequential growth every quarter,
margin expansion, and an improving mix. Some important incremental growth vectors are:
1) Boot drive storage, where SIMO is expanding from current DPU boot-drive shipments
into broader next-gen AI GPU/CPU platform content, including DPU, Ethernet, and NVLink-
related switch opportunities. We expect boot drive and other SSD solutions to account for
23%/26% of SIMO's 2026e/2027e total revenue, see more detailed analysis in AI Module:

Boot Drive Bottom-up Updates                  section.
2) Enterprise SSD / MonTitan: SIMO's enterprise SSD business, MonTitan, has started
production with two customers. The company expects to add five more Tier 1 CSPs by late

### Page 4

M
Global Idea
4
2026 and targets MonTitan to contribute 5–10% of expanded 2026 revenue on an exit
run-rate basis. However, as we see SIMO continuing to make progress, we expect its eSSD
(MonTitan) business to account for 5%/13%/19% of SIMO's total revenue in 2026e/
2027e/2028e, as besides off-the-shelf eSSD controllers, we also see the possibility for
SIMO to customize eSSD controllers for CSPs in the long run.
3) Although overall consumer demand remains lukewarm, we continue to see SIMO

gaining market share in mobile, PC and edge AI products even into 2H26. As a result, we
raise our  price target to US$400 from US$155, implying 23x 2027e EPS,  above its
historical average of 20x since 2019. We view this as justifiable as SIMO is diversifying into
more AI-exposed areas. Overall, we expect to see continual upward inflection in SIMO’s
enterprise/AI storage strategy, with 2026 the first meaningful ramp year and 2027-28 as
the scaling phase.

Fadu
Ryan Kim
We believe Fadu is gradually transitioning from a recovery story to a structurally scaling
AI storage semiconductor story. Although the company remains relatively small in
absolute revenue today, management commentary increasingly suggests the key
uncertainty is shifting away from demand visibility and toward execution/ramp timing.
Importantly, management now frames 2026 revenue of W300bn+ as effectively secured,
supported by hyperscaler-related enterprise SSD controllers, while also guiding for

meaningful operating leverage due to a controller-heavy mix and limited incremental
SG&A. The broader debate is no longer whether hyperscaler exposure exists, but rather
how far the company can penetrate AI-centric storage architectures over the next 3-5
years. The company believes it can eventually cover 3-4 of the top global hyperscalers
through indirect and direct engagement models. If realized, this could materially expand
both the revenue visibility and valuation frameworks vs. historical perceptions.

Macronix, Winbond, GigaDevice
Daniel Yen
For SLC/MLC NAND, we have seen supply tightness for a while with mainstream vendors
existing the market and only a handful of legacy players able to maintain or even increase
capacity. In addition, we are now seeing demand upside. High-density SLC NAND can
support datacenter eSSD as they have fast reading and writing speeds (link). In addition,
given the MLC NAND shortfall, enterprise HDDs are likely shifting to high-density SLC

NAND. Enterprise HDDs previously used MLC NAND for firmware, hot data and defect
mapping. We believe strong pricing momentum will continue into 4Q, following 50–60%
increases in 3Q.
We remain OW on Macronix (Top Pick; SLC/MLC NAND), Winbond (SLC NAND) and
GigaDevice (SLC NAND).

Global NAND Supply & Demand Model
In this note, we update our global NAND supply and demand model. Our calculations
indicate that AI-related NAND demand will increase by 60% YoY, or  a 9% demand-supply
gap (supply shortage) in 2027.
• We rely on ASIC/GPGPU shipment data published by our Greater China
Semiconductor team (AI Supply Chain: Preliminary 2027 TSMC CoWoS Allocation)
as the basis for our AI NAND consumption calculation.
• In practice, CSP usually has 8-16TB eSSD per ASIC  tray for the in-rack eSSD

deployment, while the externally attached/CMS-like rack can have 16-64 TB eSSD
per tray, depending on the application scenario. We group datalake-related demand
into QLC usage, which is capped by supply allocation.
• For Nvidia NAND usage, we include CSP's extra attachment in practice (which is
equivalent to 64TB per 4GPU compute node in total) when they deploy the
BlackWell rack, while other assumptions follow the company's official
configurations.

• For smartphone and PC NAND demand, we assume content will be flat while units
decrease in line with our US hardware team's model. For enterprise's eSSD usage,
we assume 10% growth YoY for both 2026 and 2027 due to AI adoption.
• For NAND supply estimates, we leverage our US SPE analyst Shane Brett's
estimates for 2026-27. He sees 27% YoY bit growth in 2027 and early signs of
NAND WFE acceleration emerging (see Raising our memory WFE forecasts (mainly

DRAM). Our 2025 supply numbers also include excessive inventory across the
supply chain carried from the last down cycle.

2028 Scenario Test
We think the biggest swing factor for 2028 and beyond could be related to YMTC. This
Chinese NAND supplier currently has Fab4 and Fab5 under construction at the same time,
with each having 100kwpm planned capacity. If they decide to dedicate all  of that to
NAND, with the announced five fabs, they could eventually take 24% market share in the
global NAND industry (see Chipflation – Navigating A Memory Crisis).

In our base case scenario, we assume 5% YoY growth for non-AI NAND demand, and the
rest of the supplier's capacity expansion to be the same as in our US Semiconductor
team's forecast. We assume AI SSD demand will grow by another 30-60% YoY in 2028 vs.
YMTC capacity ranging from 310kwpm (current base case) to 470kwpm (potential max
capacity of all announced five fabs).
In conclusion, we still see NAND supply remaining tight into 2028 if AI capex continues to

grow and YMTC keeps a more disciplined approach on expansion. However, if YMTC or
other players accelerate greenfield expansion, there will likely be potential risks of
oversupply.

What's not included in our analysis?
• Potential wafer consumption and capacity squeeze from "Super High IOPS" SSD

designed for AI inference workloads: If these new products enter volume
production in 2028, they will likely consume 3x more capacity than a normal SSD
would do, significantly tightening overall industry supply.
• New technology and AI applications that requires more SSD than we currently
estimate.
• Potential demand upside from edge AI devices, or traditional cycle recovery.

10

Channel Checks 
• 3Q26 NAND pricing is tracking at +30% QoQ for TLC eSSD while up only slightly for consumer grade NAND products due to 
pricing pressure weighing on smartphone and PC customers' margin.
• 3Q26 DRAM pricing is tracking at +20% QoQ for server grade products as consumer customers face similar pressure. Legacy 
DRAM products such as DDR3/4 tracking are much higher at +30-40% QoQ due to continued tightening supply and growing 
demand from AI related products (see Old Memory: Better to buy more)
• LTAs among memory suppliers and key major customers are still under negotiations. Micron announced in their past earnings that 
L TAs have both ceiling and floor prices that cap potential upside for pricing trends. According to our channel checks, customers are 
more willing to pay for the DRAM pricing to secure supply, while NAND pricing has received some push back.
• At the end of 2Q26, supplier inventory levels remain at historical lows. Module makers inventory levels increased significantly 
from both DRAM and NAND, consistent with their inventory building strategy. Server customers' inventory level improved 
marginally while smartphone makers are receiving more DRAM inventory. PC markers' inventory levels continued to fall for DRAM 
while remaining flat for NAND.
• According to our channel checks, inventory levels for consumer grade (PC, smartphone) memory products are elevated at some 
Chinese memory distributors, while server products remain tight. Distributors noted that pricing levels are high (and their costs 
are also high given the aggressive price hikes in the past three quarters), discouraging mid-small buyers in the market. They do not 
think demand is fundamentally getting worse, but volume shrinkage leads to pressure from holding inventories for too long. 
Based on our calculations, supply and demand dynamics will not reverse in the near term. However, increasing pricing pressure at the 
supply chain, customers' unwillingness/incapability to take higher prices and increasing levels of inventory at module makers/distributors 
indicate that mainstream consumer products pricing might hit a ceiling soon.

Module Makers: A Less Cyclical Business Model
The classic playbook:  In the past decade, the memory industry has typically faced 4-6
quarters of upcycle, with inflection when new capacity comes online to meet demand. The
cycle hasn't remained at an elevated level due to reinforced customer behavior – chase
and add more orders - hence, exaggerating the shortage in an upcycle when pricing
increases every quarter, while de-committing during the downcycle expecting pricing to be

lower in the next quarter.
Traditionally, low cost inventory plays an important role in a module maker's business
model. Module makers hoard low cost inventories at cycle troughs and sell into upcycles
with greater margin elasticity. This business model also influences module makers' share
price reactions – usually when low cost inventory is depleted, margin peaks and the stock
price corrects.
Is this time different? Under a scenario where the memory industry faces prolonged

shortage due to the AI demand boom, memory prices could remain at elevated levels for
the next 3-5 years through L TAs.  We believe this would fundamentally change the module
maker business model in a more durable upcycle as well.
1. Stable margin vs. cyclical volatility: Low cost inventory will likely be depleted at some
point this year (Longsys currently holds 2-3 quarters of inventory). With consumer
memory pricing hikes narrowing into 2H26 and seen remaining at elevated levels, margins

should gradually return to more normalized levels and become more stable than what
we've seen in previous years.
2. Limited supply: We expect muted volume growth for module makers in both 2026 and
2027 as suppliers' allocations are being shifted to CSP customers. Moving into 2028,
aggressive greenfield capacity expansion from domestic Chinese suppliers could be a
tailwind for volume growth, but at the same time, they could present challenges on
pricing support in a scenario of oversupply.

3. EPS lift through mix shift and margin expansion: In an environment where volume
growth limited and market pricing is stable, module makers would be challenged to
differentiate themselves through premium product mix shifts and new growth engines
that can increase ASPs, hence the earnings to support valuation and share price
performance.

AI Module: Boot Drive Bottom-up Updates
In our February Phison downgrade report, we published a bottom-up analysis for our boot
drive model for the growing AI market. As there is now more visibility into 2027's AI GPU,
CPU and ASIC chip shipments, together with ongoing price increases for boot drive
modules, we further update our bottom-up analysis:
Boot drive modules are a foundational, but often overlooked, component in modern

server racks, particularly in large-scale AI and cloud infrastructure. While they do not
participate directly in model training or inference workloads, boot drives are mission-
critical for system availability, orchestration and recoverability. At a high level, boot drives
provide the persistent local storage required to initialize hardware, load operating
systems, and maintain essential control-plane functions across compute nodes, switches,
and management servers within a rack.

Core Functions of Boot Drive Modules
• System Initialization and Hardware Bring-Up: Boot drives store the system
firmware, boot loader, and operating system images required to power on servers,
switches, and management nodes. Without a local boot device, hardware cannot
reliably initialize, even in disaggregated or network-booted environments.
•  Control Plane and OS Persistence: In modern AI racks, boot drives host: 1)
containerized operating systems; 2) device drivers (GPU, NIC, NVLink, accelerators);

3) system services and orchestration agents. This ensures each node can operate
autonomously and recover independently from network or storage disruptions.
•  Configuration, Logs, and Telemetry Storage: Boot drives retain system
configuration files, event logs, and telemetry data critical for debugging,
compliance and fleet-level monitoring. This function is particularly important in
hyperscale and AI factory environments where rapid fault isolation is required.

•  Fast Recovery and Operational Resilience: Local boot drives enable rapid reboot
and redeployment,  Isolation of failures to individual nodes, reduced dependency on
shared storage during recovery events. This materially improves system uptime
and operational efficiency.
While boot drives represent a smaller share of total storage capacity compared with data
cache or training storage, their ubiquity and mandatory nature make them a stable and
scalable demand driver. As system software footprints expand due to containerization,

monitoring, and local inference workloads, boot drive capacity requirements continue to
rise, increasing cumulative storage content per rack.
We try to gauge the TAM for NVIDIA's Blue Field 3 and upcoming Blue Field 4  boot drive,
based on the rack shipments we see as reasonable in the near term. We also try to size
other boot drive markets, as general servers and ASIC server racks also require boot
drives to function.
SIMO and Solidigm are the major providers of  the Blue Field 3 boot drive module.

However, we see SIMO as having 100% market share in controllers, as the other module
maker pairs its SSD with SIMO's solution. We estimate the GB200/300 server rack and
Blue Field 3 boot drive will contribute 2.3%/0.9% of total revenue to SIMO in

### Page 14

M
Global Idea
14
2026/2027e.

Vera Rubin rack, we have seen multiple module, controller and raw NAND vendors  joining
the supply for Blue Field-4 boot drives. These include, SIMO,  Phison and Realtek.
As a general server, AMD's GPU and other ASIC server racks would also require boot drive
modules/controllers inside the system, we calculate the TAM for non-boot drives needed

based on our current estimates for shipments.

SIMO: Earnings Revision Estimates, Valuation Methodology
and  Quarterly Financials
We factor in actual 1Q26 results and lift GAAP EPS by 59% and 66% for 2026e and
2027e, and introduce 2028e estimates: We factor in the better-than-expected 1Q26
result  and strong 2Q26 and 2026 full-year guidance, as the company is seeing market
share gains in the consumer tech area and enterprise SSD front. We also factor in our

estimates for the boot drive TAM analysis in this report. For gross margin, we raise our
assumptions for the SSD module business, but lower our gross margin estimates for boot
drives given the elevated raw NAND costs. The difference between GAAP and non-GAAP
earnings primarily reflects unrealized investment gains recorded in recent quarters.

### Page 22

Global Idea
22
Longsys: Estimate Revisions
We previously forecast a downcycle from 2H27 onward, with shortages persisting in 2027.
We now delay our downcycle assumption to 2H28, partially offset by volume shipment
increases in a scenario where China domestic supply catches up.
We expect Longsys to continue to enhance its product mix profile and shift memory cost
pressures through the TCM business (customers secure memory supply by themselves
while Longsys provides customized module design and assembly services).  Its R&D for the

controller business will likely further enhance its margin profile over the long term
through cost efficiency and firmware value-added services. Hence, we increase  our long-
term margin assumption to 25-38%, from the high teens previously.
As such, our 2026-28 EPS estimates increase by 299%, 247% and 244%, respectively. Our
residual income based model implied price target is now Rmb673, up from Rmb300
previously, implying 15.8x 2027e P/E.
In a bull case scenario where AI PC takes off and triggers investment sentiment on

potential business upside, we raise our scenario value to Rmb852, from Rmb435, implying
20x 2027e P/E.
In a bear case scenario where edge AI demand recovery is weaker than expected due to
shortage created from CSP capacity crowding-out effects, pricing likely remains at
elevated levels. We raise our scenario value to Rmb587, from Rmb90, implying 5.0x 2027e
P/B.

Phison: Estimate Revisions
We raise our 2026, 2027 and 2028 EPS estimates by 71%, 13% and 8%, respectively:
We factor in stronger-than-expected 2Q26 results. 2Q26 delivered a strong beat vs. our
forecasts, driven by more resilient pricing hike intervals and shipments than expected. We
expect 3Q26 to be the peak of the year, supported by more support from Kioxia’s dummy
die, leading to around 20% QoQ revenue growth and a better-than-feared (flat Q/Q)

margin. In 4Q26, we expect emerging weakness from consumer tech and a more moderate
pricing environment to result in 4Q revenue declining 20% QoQ, with margins normalizing
to ~50%. Reflecting the better pricing environment seen for 2026, this leads to stronger-
than-expected profitability in 2027, partially offset by the ongoing R&D investments.

Phison: Valuation Methodology
We raise our price target from NT$2,248 to NT$2,588, reflecting our estimate changes.
Our other RI parameters are unchanged, including a cost of equity of 9.0%, a medium-
term growth rate of 9.0%, a terminal growth rate of 4.0% and a dividend payout ratio
assumption of 51.2%. We increase our bull and bear case values from NT$3,090 and NT
$1,125 to NT$3,400 and NT$1,250, respectively, implying 10x 2026e and 26x 2027e EPS in

the bull case and 4x 2026e and 9x 2027e EPS in the bear.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O or Over) - The stock's total return is expected to exceed the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry
team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis over the next 12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E or Equal) - The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or

industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis over the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR) - Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's
industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U or Under) - The stock's total return is expected to be below the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry

team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場

證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

1 
3563 牧德(Machvision)

報告日期： 2026/07/01  研 究 員：翁浩軒 (Davy Wong) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 半導體檢測為新成長機會  
牧德近年積極切入半導體市場 ， 投入 Wafer AVI 、 Package AVI 、 Auto 
OM 、 高階 IC 載板等設備開剸 。 牧德 Wafer AVI 自動外觀檢查系統 ，
可有效檢測晶圓之切割碎裂、bump/pad 刮傷、異色、遺失、偏移、
變形及外物等，目前已出貨半導體客戶; Package AVI 大尺寸產品
預計 4Q26 送樣認證; Auto OM 產品已送樣，年底有望接到訂單。
牧德除了受惠於日月光集團之擴產，亦將業務拓展至其他客戶及
市場。

二.  2Q26 營收雙位數成長，預估 EPS 5.64 元 
2Q26 營收預期穩步成長，主要因 PCB 設備出貨逐步認列，加上半
導體比重提升 ， 5 月營收 3.49(MoM+1.68% 、 YoY+5.14%) ， 預期 2Q26
營收 1 0 . 5 1 億( Q o Q + 2 1 . 7 %、Y o Y + 8 . 4 7 % )，稅後淨利 3 . 6 1 億
(QoQ+22.41%、YoY+47.94%)，EPS 5.64 元。

三.接單暢旺，預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 24.06 元/36.01 元 
展望 2026 年，AI 伺服器與 HPC 帶動高階 PCB 及半導體檢測設備
需求，牧德接單暢旺，訂單能見度已達 2027 上半年。公司在 PCB 
外觀檢測維持全球領先地位，除了 AI 帶動 PCB 硬板需求外，光通
訊傳輸對光模塊需求，進而帶動 mSAP 製程設備需求，牧德光模
塊硬板 AOI 訂單超過 20 億，預期相關設備 4Q26 開始出貨。半導
體業務方面，半導體新品完成認證逐步貢獻營收、接單能見度優
於去年同期，集團大幅擴產將帶動 Wafer AVI 設備需求。預估牧德
2026 年 營 收 42.16 億 元 (YoY+32.1%) ， 稅 後 淨 利 15.39 億 元
(YoY+52.28%) ， EPS 24.06 元 。 預 估 2027 年 營 收 58.21 億 元
(YoY+38.07%) ，稅後淨利 23.03 億元(YoY+49.65%) ，EPS 36.01 元。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 900 元 
牧德長期深耕 PCB AOI 及 AVI 檢測設備市場 ， 產品具備線路檢測 、
外觀檢測及 2D/3D 量測技術之公司，具有整合應用能力。隨著電
子產品走向輕薄短小且對品質要求提升，PCB、半導體產業採用
AOI 設備以降低人力成本費用之趨勢不變。考量到 1)AI 帶動 PCB
及半導體 AOI 設備需求，公司訂單能見度高、2027 年獲利創高，
參考 2027 年 EPS 給予 25 倍本益比，目標價 900 元，建議買進。

證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

2 
貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
牧德成立於 1998 年 6 月，2009 年策略轉向至檢測設備研發，並成
為國內最大的 PCB 自動光學檢測設備廠，具備一條龍檢測解決方
案的能力，主要提供 PCB 機械視覺檢測及量測系統設備之製造、
研發及銷售 ， 產品包含 PCB 鑽孔與成型製程量測與檢測系列 、 PCB 
線路檢查系列及 HDI 與 IC 載板檢查系列及外觀檢測系列 ， 2023
年 6 月日月光半導體以每股新台 161.50 元取得其私募普通股 1341
萬 8000 股，對牧德持股比例 23.1%，成為第一大股東，並與光學
檢測領域的鏵友益合作共同佈局半導體封裝及先進封裝 AOI 設備
開發。公司專精技術分為三大部分，第一為外觀檢測(AVI)，第二
為 2D 及 3D 量測(2D/3D measurement) ， 第三為線路檢查(線路 AOI) ，
三項技術可廣範應用在半導體及 PCB 產業。主要客戶包含國內外
PCB 廠 、 載板廠 、 半導體封測廠 。 主要同業為 KLA 、 Onto 、 Camtek 、
Screen、德律、由田、晶彩科等。

二.2026 年營運概況及展望  
1. 半導體檢測為長期成長機會 
牧德過往以 PCB 檢測設備為主，近年積極切入半導體市場，日月
光 2023 年入股牧德取 23%股權成為單一最大股東，並進入董事
會，牧德將藉此投入技術層次更高的半導體設備，投入領域包括
Wafer AVI、Package AVI、Auto OM、高階 IC 載板等檢測。牧德
Wafer AVI 自動外觀檢查系統，可有效檢測晶圓之切割碎裂、
bump/pad 刮傷、異色、遺失、偏移、變形及外物等，目前已出貨
半導體客戶; Package AVI 大尺寸產品預計 4Q26 送樣認證; Auto OM
產品已送樣，年底有望接到訂單。牧德從原先 PCB 領域拓展到半
導體，除了受惠於日月光集團之擴產，亦將業務拓展至其他客戶
及市場。目前半導體營收比重 15%，預估 2027 年比重將提升至
20~30%。

2. 1Q26 獲利符合預期，EPS 4.61 元 
1Q26 營收 8.63 億(QoQ+30.12%、YoY+9.02%)，在去年營收高基期
下 YoY 仍持續成長，主要受惠於 AI 帶動 PCB 擴產需求，毛利率
60.58%符合預期，不過低於去年同期，主要因產品組合，加上部
分原材料成本增加影響，費用控制得宜，稅後淨利 2 . 9 5 億
(QoQ+23.49%、YoY-2.9%)，EPS 4.61 元。

3. 2Q26 營收雙位數成長，預估 EPS 5.64 元 
2Q26 營收預期穩步成長，主要因 PCB 設備出貨逐步認列，加上半

4. 2026 年接單暢旺，預估 EPS 24.06 元 
展望 2026 年，AI 伺服器與 HPC 帶動高階 PCB 需求成長，加上先
進封裝推升半導體檢測設備需求、半導體製程精密帶動 AOI 市場
擴大，帶動牧德接單暢旺，訂單能見度已達 2027 上半年。公司在
PCB 外觀檢測維持全球領先地位，市占 8 成以上，除了 AI 帶動
PCB 硬板需求外，光通訊傳輸對光模塊需求，進而帶動 mSAP （改
良型半加成法）PCB 製程設備需求，牧德在細線路細且高密度的
光模塊檢測市場具技術優勢，目前看到 2027 年光模塊硬板 AOI 訂
單超過 20 億，預期相關設備 4Q26 開始出貨。半導體業務方面，
半導體新品完成認證逐步貢獻營收、接單能見度優於去年同期，
集團大幅擴產將帶動 Wafer AVI 設備需求 。 公司因應原材料成本上
漲，設備報價調漲 5~10%，預估下半年價格調漲加上產品組合轉
佳，有助毛利率改善。預估牧德 2 0 2 6 年營收 4 2 . 1 6 億元
(YoY+32.1%)，毛利率 62.4%，稅後淨利 15.39 億元(YoY+52.28%)，
EPS 24.06 元。預估 2027 年營收 58.21 億元(YoY+38.07%)，毛利率
63.36%，稅後淨利 23.03 億元(YoY+49.65%)，EPS 36.01 元。

散熱及低軌道衛星雙引擎 
 Radiator 營收逐季放量，2026 年營收倍增:GB200/GB300 NVL72
機櫃發熱量約為 140~150 kw， 一台IT Rack 需要搭配 2~3 台 Sidecar
來進行液態對氣態的散熱，一台 Sidecar 內部配置 1~3 組 Radiator
與高效能風扇，聯德 1H26 Radiator 單月出貨約 1100~1200 組，現
有廠區月產能配置為 1500~2000 組，逐季放量，2026 年營收倍增。 
 業界最小自主回心功能浮動接頭 ，2H26 即將出貨:公司亦佈局多項散
熱產品，如交換器水冷板及浮動接頭等，交換器水冷板面積較 CPU
及 GPU 小很多，ASP 與面積為同向關係，然此應用對聯德而言仍為
新營收貢獻。另外，我們認為浮動接頭為公司另一個看點，其浮動接
頭軸向及徑向浮動量優於現有設計，同時亦開發出業界最小自主回心
功能 ，自主回心功能是浮動接頭中一項重要自動校正機制 ， 這項功能
可以強制讓連接部位自動回到中心對正位置 ， 以減少對接時快接頭漏
水問題。聯德產品已通過客戶驗證，預計 2H26 開始出貨。 
 給予買進評等，目標價 150 元：散熱及低軌道衛星高成長引擎，目
前預估數字不含浮動接頭，可視為未來上修空間，Space X 概念股應
享有高評價，預估 2026 年 EPS 6. 98 元，2027 年 EPS 10.37 元，

聯德為國內精密金屬沖壓零組件
製造商 
 聯德為國內精密金屬沖壓零組件製造商 
聯德控股股份有限公司成立於 2003 年，主要從事精密金屬沖壓零組件的研發、
生產與銷售，產品包括通過沖壓成形製程的各種電子產品散熱模組、電子產品
零組件、汽車零組件與互聯健身設備組裝件，同時為汽車與電子產品生產廠家
提供沖壓模具的配套。

聯德 營收佔比

Sources : 法說會、MasterLink

汽 車 零 組 件 每 年 營 收
YOY+5~8%，穩健成長

汽車零組件每年營收 YOY+5~8%，穩健成長 
聯德 2025 年營收比重為汽車零組件 55.67%，電子零組件 38.73%，互聯健身
器材 5.09%，其他 0.51%。汽車零組件主要銷售市場為中國，再者為歐洲，目
前營收占比燃油車及新能源車各半，受惠於新能源車銷售成長，公司所提供之
安全零部件包括安全氣囊、安全帶、天窗等零組件持續成長，過往僅以零組件
出貨，現在可做到部分零組件模組化，預估汽車零組件每年營收 YOY+5~8%，
穩健成長。

Radiator 營收逐季放量 ，2026 年
營收倍增

Radiator 營收逐季放量，2026 年營收倍增 
散 熱 業 務 逐 漸 開 花 結 果: 目 前 公 司 散 熱 產 品 以 散 熱 排 模 組(Radiator) 為 主 ，
Sidecar 搭配 Radiator 的 數 量 會 依 照 機 櫃 功 耗 與 散 熱 設 計 而 定 ， 以
GB200/GB300 NVL72 來看，因機櫃發熱量約為 140~150 kw，故一台 IT Rack
需要搭配 2~3 台 Sidecar 來進行液態對氣態的散熱 ， 一台Sidecar 內部配置 1~3
組 Radiator 與高效能風扇，熱水流進 Radiator 密密麻麻的金屬細管中，透過無
數片超薄金屬鰭片(Fin)，將熱量瞬間傳導到 Radiator 表面，並透過風扇吹走熱
量已達到水對氣解熱。聯德 1H26 Radiator 單月出貨約 1100~1200 組，直接/
間接出貨對象 為 CSP 廠 之組裝廠，現有八德廠 及 中國廠區月產能配置 為
1500~2000 組，4Q25 已經開始出貨，逐季放量，2026 年營收倍增。

業 界 最 小 自 主 回 心 功 能 浮 動 接
頭，2H26 即將出貨

2026 年低軌道衛星相關產品營收
倍數成長 
業界最小自主回心功能浮動接頭，2H26 即將出貨 
公司亦佈局多項散熱產品，如交換器水冷板及浮動接頭等，交換器水冷板面積
較 CPU 及 GPU 小很多，ASP 與面積為同向關係，然此應用對聯德而言仍為新
營收貢獻。另外，我們認為浮動接頭為公司另一個看點， 其浮動接頭軸向及徑
向浮動量優於現有 設計，同時亦開發出業界最小自主回心功能，自主回心功能
(Autonomic Centering) 是浮動接頭中一項重要自動校正機制 ，當氣壓缸或是油
壓缸的推桿與被驅動的機械結構間因為安裝誤差產能偏心或是偏角時，這項功
能可以強制讓連接部位自動回到中心對正位置，以減少對接時快接頭漏水問
題。聯德產品已通過客戶驗證，預計 2H26 開始出貨。

低軌道衛星供貨產品為 Space X 地面收發站之沖壓件 ，2Q26 產能已開出 ， 逐月
將拉高產能，目前由中國昆山、泰國及墨西哥為主要生產基地，預計第二季末
逐月將拉高產能，去年基期較低，2026 年營收將倍數成長。

給予買進，目標價 150 元

給予買進評等，目標價 150 元 
散熱及低軌道衛星高成長引擎，目前預估數字不含浮動接頭，可視為未來上修
空間，Space X 概念股應享有高評價 ，預估 2026 年 EPS 6.98 元，2027 年 EPS 
10.37 元，目標價 150 元。

風險提示 
出貨不如預期，ASP 不如預期。

遠雄 (5522 TT)  Trading Buy

北士科新案 -遠雄明玥銷售逾 40% 。  
投資建議 ：考量交屋展望優於預期，研究部預估 2026 年稅後
EPS8.59 元，預 估2027 年有望配發 6.2 元 現 金 股 利，以06/25/2026
收盤價換算，現金殖利率達 9.4% 。受惠 Nvidia 台灣總部落腳北
士科，明玥推出不足 1 季，即售出逾 40% ，銷售表現亮眼。巨
蛋投資展望亦優於預期，且評價尚處低檔。給予投資建議
Trading Buy ，目標價 72 元 (2026 年 PBR1.06X) 。  
北士科案 -明玥推案不足一季，賣破 4 成：遠雄明玥 (總銷 90 億
元，銷售率 43%) ，推案後不到 1 個季度，即售出超過 40% 。
Nvidia 台灣總部落腳北士科，不僅帶來逾 10,000 個工作機會，
更吸引高資產客群等買盤進駐。  
巨蛋投資展望優於預期，有望提前達損平： 公司表示，目前遠
雄巨蛋辦公入駐率已逾 80% ，巨蛋公司招租表現近乎滿租，優
於預期。觀察 1~2Q/26 以來自由現金流表現佳，整體營運狀況
亦優於預期。研究部認為，考量巨蛋營運展望正向，預估巨蛋
投資有望於 1H27 前達成損平。  
01~05/2026 累計營收 YoY+643.57% ：遠雄 05/2026 營收 6.47 億
元， MoM+182.24% 、 YoY+191.64% ，主要來自成屋銷售及租賃
收入認列； 01~05/2026 累計營收 65.39 億元， YoY+643.57% 。  
2026 年交屋展望優於預期，上修預估稅後 EPS 至 8.59 元：2026
年新完工案包括仰森、洄山行、綠美、綠禾、東御苑、琢蘊、
Park 16 等平均銷售率達 90%， 其中綠禾已於 06/2026 認列入帳 。
洄山行及綠美因 3Q26 取得使照、 4Q26 啟動交屋，惟考量銷售
表現及公司展現積極交屋決心，預估分別有望入帳 50%、100%。
其餘個案預計 07~10/2026 陸續交屋入帳， 2Q26 將為 2026 年營
收谷底， 3〜 4Q/26 為認列高峰期。研究部預估遠雄 2026 年營收
為 344.45 億元， YoY+10.57% ，稅後純益為 67.17 億元，
YoY+15.32% ，稅後 EPS 為 8.59 元。

2 群益投顧 
  遠雄建設 ESG ：  
遠雄建設以 NEX Community 未來共生為永續願景，透過美好生活、環境永
續及數位智慧三大策略，全力推動企業永續發展目標，並積極接軌聯合國
2030 永續發展目標中 9 項主題，以美好共生為品牌主張，致力於創造永續
共享的生活環境。環境永續方面，致力於低碳轉型、推動綠建築、溫室氣體
管理、節能措施、資源循環，及綠色營運；社會責任方面，公司自 2016 起
成立專案公益信託，並協助衛服部北區兒童之家購置全新床具；公司治理方
面，連續 8 年發行永續報告書，採用 GRI、SASB 、TCFD 等國際標準，完整
揭露 ESG 重要議題，並於 2022 年參與 TCSA 永續報告書評選，榮獲第 15
屆不動產及營造業第一類銀獎。  
公司簡介 ：  
遠雄建設成立於 1978 年，為台灣市值第二大之建設開發公司，實收資本額
78.16 億元，具備規模經濟、垂直整合、低成本土地庫存、附加價值，及豐
富經驗之經營團隊等五大優勢，並藉由工地計畫 1.0 轉型至工地計畫 3.0 ，
發揮循環經濟。公司致力於以品牌價值創造差異化，並維持穩健財務槓桿
度。永續 ESG 方面，在 2023 年榮獲 TCSA 台灣企業永續獎第 1 類金級獎，
並於近十年已取得逾 30 件之內政部綠建築標章。  
公司持股與在手開發案 ：  
持有遠雄巨蛋 28%、三新奧特萊斯 30%，投資於固定收益之台北大巨蛋園區
與 OUTLET 商城，及遠雄營造 100% 的股份，將提供穩定現金流。目前在手
開發案中北部占 41%、南部占 40%、中部占 19%。未來幾年開發案完工案量
分別為 2026 年 328 億元、 2027 年 325 億元、 2028 年 265 億元、 2029 年 449
億元、 2030 年後 1,723 億元，土地庫存相當充裕。相較 03/2026 法說，公司
表示未來商辦營收佔比期望進一步提升至 30% ，並持續增加桃園以北商廠
辦案量比重，以 70% 住宅佔比為目標。土地購置選擇方面，將全盤考慮地
段、產品選擇及創造高附加價值，並以滿足剛性需求客群等為首要要件。

3 群益投顧 
選擇性信用管制落底，軟著陸可期：  
央行總裁楊金龍於 06/10/2026 立法院財委會中，提前表態選擇性信用管制
政策到此為止。研究部認為，自 1Q26 以來中古屋市場開價已明顯滑落，加
上預售單價以過去過度飆漲區域如中南部持續下修中，且成交量亦處於低
檔盤整區間，成交量落底跡象已現。 03/2026 底國銀不動產放款集中度比率
續降至 35.56% ，低於央行提出之期望水準 36%，且銀行法天條亦連續 11 個
月下滑，增加銀行房貸承做意願。 1H26 股市交投熱絡，財富效果亦可望提
升國民進場看屋動機。維持 2026 年房市價修量微增預期。  
公司表示，當前市況仍以北部表現最穩，其次為中部、南部。觀察自 03/2026
釋出第 2 戶購屋貸款成數上限提升至 60% 後， 04~05/2026 受惠北士科及板
橋新推案，銷售狀況較 1Q26 提升 3 倍之多。截至 05/2026 底，個案銷售進
程與原訂計畫落差不大。另外， 2026 年因過去推案量大增， 2 房產品空置率
有所提升，惟 3 房產品需求似有復甦跡象。  
土方影響收斂，但造價成本回不去了：  
針對土方議題，公司表示以各區域來看，北部影響有限、中部影響幾乎沒有，
南部受到的衝擊最大。整體來看，土方成本約提升總造價成本逾 5,000 元 /
坪。 2026 年亦因機電成本上揚，北部及中部均有顯著提升。南部雖受供需
結構不同略為下滑，惟因個案規劃多屬 15 樓以上高樓層，單坪造價成本仍
逾 18 萬元。因此，多方因素均導致預售開價欲降不易。  
未來股利配發率仍將持穩逾 70% ：  
經董事會通過，已配發 2H25 現金股利 5.5 元，優於原預期之 5.2 元。 2025
年度盈餘配發率約 74% ，以 06/25/2026 收盤價推算，現金殖利率約 8.3% 。
公司表示，未來預計仍將維持 70% 以上盈餘配發率及現金股利半年配機制。

4 群益投顧 
2026 年交屋展望優於預期，上修預估稅後 EPS 至 8.59 元：  
2026 年預期可認列建案共 9 筆，其中包括台中遠雄幸福城 (總銷 190 億元 )、
北投遠雄商舟 (總銷 95 億元 )等兩筆成屋預估均於 2026 年全數認列，其中商
舟已認列入帳。  
桃園遠雄仰森 (總銷 53 億元，銷售率 100%) ，已取得使照。  
台中遠雄洄山行 (總銷 38 億元，銷售率 32%) ，預計 3Q26 初取得使照。  
台中遠雄綠美 (總銷 25 億元，銷售率 99%) ，預計 08/2026 底取得使照。  
台南遠雄綠禾 (總銷 36 億元，銷售率 98%) ，已取得使照。  
台南遠雄東御苑 (總銷 33 億元，銷售率 100%) ，已於 06/2026 前取得使照。
高雄遠雄琢蘊 (總銷 79 億元，銷售率 98%) ，已於 06/2026 前取得使照。  
高雄遠雄 Park 16( 總銷 32 億元，銷售率 100%) ，已於 06/2026 前取得使照。
2026 年新完工案包括仰森、洄山行、綠美、綠禾、東御苑、琢蘊、 Park 16
等平均銷售率達 90% ，其中綠禾已於 06/2026 認列入帳。洄山行及綠美因
3Q26 取得使照、 4Q26 啟動交屋，惟考量銷售表現及公司展現積極交屋決
心，預估分別有望交屋 50%、 100% 。其餘個案預計 07~10/2026 陸續交屋入
帳， 2Q26 將為 2026 年營收谷底， 3〜 4Q/26 為認列高峰期。研究部上修預
估遠雄 2026 年營收為 344.45 億元， YoY+10.57% ，稅後純益為 67.17 億元，
YoY+15.32% ，稅後 EPS 為 8.59 元。若以 70~75% 現金配發率中緣為基礎，
預估 2027 年有望配發現金股利 6.2 元，以 06/25/2026 收盤價換算，現金殖
利率達 9.4% 。  
2027 年入帳高峰期在 2H27 ：  
2027 年預期仍有望入帳 8 筆建案 ， 包括遠雄迴山行(總銷 38 億元 )遞延交屋 。
新北遠雄信義 Center( 總銷 31 億元，銷售率 33%) ，預計 01/2027 取得使照。
桃園遠雄 MetaLink( 總銷 46 億元，銷售率 7%) ， 1Q26 推案。  
台中遠雄敦富 (總銷 54 億元，銷售率 66%) ，預計 07/2027 取得使照。  
台中遠雄星呈 (總銷 79 億元，銷售率 68%) ，預計 07/2027 取得使照。  
台南遠雄山禾 (總銷 62 億元，銷售率 65%) ，預計 2026 年底取得使照。  
高雄遠雄峰蘊 (總銷 90 億元，銷售率 71%) ，預計 08/2027 取得使照。  
高雄遠雄沐蘊 (總銷 40 億元，銷售率 62%) ，預計 09/2027 取得使照。  
2027 年新完工案大多數將於 2~3Q/27 取得使照，預估 2027 年交屋入帳高峰
期為 3~4Q/27 。研究部預估遠雄 2027 年營收為 267.22 億元， YoY-22.42% ，
稅後純益為 47.22 億元， YoY-29.70% ，稅後 EPS 為 6.04 元。

5 群益投顧 
巨蛋投資展望優於預期，有望提前達損平 ：  
遠雄巨蛋國際中心之辦公大樓租金收入逾 4,000 元 /坪，已於 1Q25 正式入
駐，其中秀泰影城、 SOGO 百貨及洲際飯店旅館預計分別於 11/2025 、1Q26、
2Q26 開幕。經營特許期經仲裁後，延長 5 年至 03/2062 。  
公司表示，儘管 1Q26 因營運初期，每年仍需依直線法攤銷折舊費用，損益
表仍呈虧損狀態。惟目前遠雄巨蛋辦公入駐率已逾 80%，巨蛋公司招租表現
近乎滿租，優於預期。觀察 1~2Q/26 以來自由現金流表現佳，整體營運狀況
亦優於預期。  
研究部認為，遠雄建設依其持有巨蛋股份換算， 2026 年雖因商場、飯店及
影城開幕後，墊高折舊費用，預估損平難度仍高。惟考量營運展望正向，加
上公司對巨蛋貢獻固定投資收益信心十足，以及租金、演唱會及運動賽事等
收入同步挹注，預估巨蛋投資有望於 1H27 前達成損平。  
北士科案 -明玥推案不足一季，賣破 4 成：  
遠雄明玥 (總銷 90 億元，銷售率 43%) 為 2Q26 新推案。推案後不到 1 個季
度，即售出超過 40% ，銷售表現顯著優於同業平均。顯示 Nvidia 台灣總部
落腳北士科，不僅帶來逾 10,000 個工作機會，更吸引高資產客群等買盤進
駐。

投資建議 ：  
考量交屋展望優於預期，研究部預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 至 8.59 元，預估 2027

9 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

5530 龍巖(LUNGYEN)

報告日期： 2026/06/26 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 預估塔位墓園 2026 年營收 13.00 億元 ，YoY-22.9% 
塔位與墓園 01~05/2026 營收金額 6.59 億元，YoY-32.28% 。預期
2026 年，高雄光之丘己於取得建物使照，但內部設施需要 2~3 季
度才完成，屆時才可貢獻營收，時間點接近年底，加上需完款才
能認識，宏遠先暫不估計光之丘貢獻，除光之丘外尚有部分完工
完款金額，加上現貨銷售金額挹注，預估塔位與墓園 2026 年營收
金額 13 億元， YoY-22.9% 。 
二. 預估喪葬服務 2026 年營收 21.00 億元 ，YoY+3.3% 
喪葬服務 01~05/2026 營收金額 8.74 億元 ，YoY+3.16%。預 期2026
年，根據國發會預估 2026 年死亡人數落在 20.1~20.2 萬人間，較
2025 年增加 0.35~0.8% ，龍巖持續在市占率提升帶動服務件數增
加，另外更添金額持續增加，宏遠預估喪葬服務 2026 年營收金額
21 億元， YoY+3.3% 。 
三. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 2.60 元 
龍巖 2026 年雖然禮儀服務與租賃收入增加 ， 但受到高雄光之丘於
2026 年底完工，貢獻營收機率低，預期龍巖 2026 年營運將呈現
衰退格局。預估龍巖 2026 年營收金額 38.6 億元， YoY-4.62% ，
預估營業利益 8.74 億元 ，YoY-1.09% ， 預估稅後盈餘10.91 億元 ，
YoY-5.48% ，預估稅後 EPS 2.60 元。  
四. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 3.00 元 
龍巖 2027 年在禮儀服務穩定成長外 ， 塔墓業務受惠高雄光之丘完
工完款開始挹注營運，帶動龍巖 2027 年營運恢復成長動能。預估
龍巖 2027 年營收金額 43.00 億元， YoY+11.4% ，預估營業利益
10.41 億元， YoY+19.17% ，預估稅後盈餘 12.62 億元，
YoY+15.64% ，預估稅後 EPS 3.00 元。  
五. 投資建議區間操作，區間價 43~57 元 
觀察 2021~2025 年國內死亡人數 18.44 萬人， 20.7 萬人， 20.54
萬人，20.21 萬人與 20.03 萬人，死亡人數相較出生人數已有逐年
增加趨勢，將有助龍巖禮儀服務營收成長。龍巖 2016~2024 年
PER 10~24X ，龍巖 2026 年 PER 19.1X ，2027 年 PER 16.6X ，
評價合理水準 ， 故投資建議區間操作 ， 區間價43~57 元。(16.5~22X 
2026 年稅後 EPS)

投資評等：區間操作 
操作區間：43~57 元 
     16.5~22X 2026 EPS 
參考價位：48.25 元 
     18.6X 2026 EPS

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
龍巖為國內第一家掛牌上櫃的殯葬公司，資本額 42.01 億元，龍
巖在 2010 年初與大漢建設股份換股， 02/2011 大漢與龍巖進行合
併。龍巖有別於國內其他殯葬公司，採取殯與葬整合服務。在殯
方面，國內主要競爭對手為萬安生命；在葬方面，國內主要競爭
對手為金寶山。觀察國內死亡人數相較出生人數已有逐年增加趨
勢，有助龍巖禮儀服務與塔位墓園業務未來營運動能成長。

圖一、龍巖核心競爭力

資料來源：龍巖人本 &宏遠投顧彙整

二. 2026 年營運概況及展望

預估 2026 年塔位與墓園營收金額 13.00 億元， YoY-22.9%

龍巖在全台墓園主要有新北市三芝真龍殿、萬里區福田妙國、桃
園富岡陵園、中部地區台中太平寶山陵園、嘉義水上嘉雲陵園與
高雄內門安泰陵園、花園吉安花蓮蓮園。

靈骨塔主要分成預售產品與現貨產品，其中預售產品認列方式為
在完工後，箱架裝設完成即可認列收入，現貨產品如土葬區土地
或宇綺建設持有現有塔位，只要完成過戶動作即可認列營收。

預估 2026 年喪葬服務營收 21 億元， YoY+3.3%

喪葬服務方面，分為現貨市場與期貨市場兩種；現貨市場售價約
在 35 萬元之水準，生前契約之期貨市場售價則約 20~25 萬元。
喪葬服務收入即為生前契約，在實際履約時就可認列營收。龍巖
在國內市佔率 3.5~4% ，在品牌逐漸獲得認同下，未來五年市佔率
將提升至 10% 。觀察龍巖生前契約市占率己由 2018 年的 74.8%
逐年提升至 2023 年 79% ，上述顯示，龍巖市場占有率己逐步提
升中。在國內高齡化趨勢推升死亡人口維持高檔水準，在服務件
數持續增加 1~2% ，加上龍巖市占率略為增加，喪葬服務 2023 年
營收金額 19.79 億元， YoY+4.0% 。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 2.60 元

龍巖 2026 年雖然禮儀服務與租賃收入增加 ， 但受到高雄光之丘於
2026 年底完工，貢獻營收機率低，預期龍巖 2026 年營運將呈現
衰退格局。預估龍巖 2026 年營收金額 38.6 億元， YoY-4.62% ，
預估營業利益 8.74 億元 ，YoY-1.09% ， 預估稅後盈餘10.91 億元 ，
YoY-5.48% ，預估稅後 EPS 2.60 元。

二. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 3.00 元

龍巖 2027 年在禮儀服務穩定成長外 ， 塔墓業務受惠高雄光之丘完
工完款開始挹注營運，帶動龍巖 2027 年營運恢復成長動能。預估
龍巖 2027 年營收金額 43.00 億元， YoY+11.4% ，預估營業利益
10.41 億元， YoY+19.17% ，預估稅後盈餘 12.62 億元，
YoY+15.64% ，預估稅後 EPS 3.00 元。

   公司簡介 
聯茂為 CCL 供應商，產品主要應用於消費性電子、基礎建設、智慧型手機、車用電子
等領域。  
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 400 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，390 
【大盤指數/股價】44572 / 346.5

➢ 1Q26 原物料成本上漲，侵蝕毛利率表現 
聯茂 1Q26 營收 91.4 億元，QoQ+2.5%，YoY+20.6%，1Q26 營收達
成率 103%，符合預期；毛利率 11.4%，低於國票預估 15.1%，主因原
物料成本上漲，公司尚未即時反映漲價；EPS 0.87 元，QoQ-26.6%，
YoY-6.6%，低於國票預估 1.29 元。 
➢ 伺服器平台進入換代週期，帶動材料等級提升 
聯茂 05/2026 營收 38.03 億元，MoM+8.2%，YoY+29.3%，2Q26 營
收達成率 65%，符合預期，因應原物料成本上漲，公司啟動產品漲價，
整體調漲幅度 20%以上，逐季漲價將成為常態，將體現在營收成長以及
毛利率回穩；稅率方面，由於公司持續將中國廠的海外盈餘匯回台灣，
預估有效稅率將維持在 35%的高檔區間，短期內不會明顯下滑。國票預
估聯茂 2Q26 營收 112.9 億元，QoQ+23.5%，YoY+27.2%；毛利率
14.1%；EPS 1.79 元，QoQ+107%，YoY+54.9%。 
展望未來 ， 公司主要成長動能來自基礎建設 ，2H26 伺服器平台進入換代
週期，Intel 平台將升級至支援 PCIe Gen 6，AMD 亦將跟進升級，CPU
主板材料將由 M6 升級至 M7，挹注產品組合優化，對毛利率帶來正面
貢獻；AI 伺服器方面，公司持續供應副板材料，等級為 M4~M6 不等，
ASIC 應用方面，小量供應 M7~M8 等級材料，持續在 M9、M10 等級
材料進行送樣認證，佈局中長期產品組合；產能方面，受限玻纖布嚴重
缺料，儘管訂單滿載，目前產能利用率約 75%，預估將隨著來料狀況有
所提升，預計泰國一廠 4Q26 開出月產能 30 萬張，泰國二廠亦開始擴
建，預計 1H28 陸續開出，中長期有望隨著產能利用率上升、新產能開
出帶動營運規模提升。國票預估聯茂 2026 年/2027 年營收 479.8 億元
/756.2 億元，YoY+44.97%/+57.6%；毛利率 15.1%/19.6%；EPS 8.14
元/20.20 元，YoY+95.8%/+148.2%。 
➢ 調升至買進評等，目標價 400 元 
2H26 伺服器平台進入換代週期 ， 將帶動聯茂產品組合升級 ， 中高階CCL
需求持續強勁，公司將隨著產能利用率上升、新產能開出帶動營運規模
提升，參考過去 PE 區間上緣 20X，調升至買進評等，目標價 400 元
(2027F PE 20X)。

1Q26 原物料成本上漲，侵蝕毛利率表現 
聯茂 1Q26 營收 91.4 億元，QoQ+2.5%，YoY+20.6%，1Q26 營收達成率 103%，
符合預期；毛利率 11.4%，低於國票預估 15.1%，主因原物料成本上漲，公司尚未即
時反映漲價；EPS 0.87 元，QoQ-26.6%，YoY-6.6%，低於國票預估 1.29 元。 
1Q26 產品組合：消費性電子 9%、基礎建設 72%、智慧型手機 5%、車用電子 14%。

伺服器平台進入換代週期，帶動材料等級提升 
聯茂 05/2026 營收 38.03 億元 ，MoM+8.2%，YoY+29.3%，2Q26 營收達成率 65%，
符合預期，因應原物料成本上漲，公司啟動產品漲價，整體調漲幅度 20%以上，逐季
漲價將成為常態，將體現在營收成長以及毛利率回穩；稅率方面，由於公司持續將中
國廠的海外盈餘匯回台灣，預估有效稅率將維持在 35%的高檔區間，短期內不會明顯
下滑。國票預估聯茂 2Q26 營收 112.9 億元，QoQ+23.5%，YoY+27.2%；毛利率
14.1%；EPS 1.79 元，QoQ+107%，YoY+54.9%。 
展望未來，公司主要成長動能來自基礎建設，2H26 伺服器平台進入換代週期，Intel
平台將升級至支援 PCIe Gen 6，AMD 亦將跟進升級，CPU 主板材料將由 M6 升級至
M7，挹注產品組合優化，對毛利率帶來正面貢獻；AI 伺服器方面，公司持續供應副
板材料，等級為 M4~M6 不等，ASIC 應用方面，小量供應 M7~M8 等級材料，持續
在 M9、M10 等級材料進行送樣認證，佈局中長期產品組合；產能方面，受限玻纖布
嚴重缺料，儘管訂單滿載，目前產能利用率約 75%，預估將隨著來料狀況有所提升，
預計泰國一廠 4Q26 開出月產能 30 萬張，泰國二廠亦開始擴建，預計 1H28 陸續開
出，中長期有望隨著產能利用率上升、新產能開出帶動營運規模提升。國票預估聯茂
2026 年/2027 年營收 479.8 億元/756.2 億元， YoY+44.97%/+57.6%；毛利率
15.1%/19.6%；EPS 8.14 元/20.20 元，YoY+95.8%/+148.2%。 
調升至買進評等，目標價 400 元 
2H26 伺服器平台進入換代週期，將帶動聯茂產品組合升級，中高階 CCL 需求持續強
勁，公司將隨著產能利用率上升、新產能開出帶動營運規模提升，參考過去 PE 區間
上緣 20X，調升至買進評等，目標價 400 元(2027F PE 20X)。

產品組合

股價相對大盤走勢

GIS-KY(6456 TT)  Trading Buy

考量 AR glass 、光通訊題材，投資建議為 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ： 雖然記憶體缺料影響消費性電子景氣 、 Sharp 4Q26 關
閉大尺寸面板產線均不利於 GIS-KY 2026 年本業表現，不過，
GIS-KY 與最大客戶共同開發 AR glass 、切入光通訊領域均為重
要股價題材，故群益對 GIS-KY 的投資建議為 Trading Buy 。  
估計 2Q26 營收 QoQ-22.13% ：1Q26 營收與 4Q25 相去有限，不
過，毛利率受到產品結構影響而降低至 4.98%(4Q25 為 5.73%) ，
本業虧損擴大至 3.21 億元，稅後虧損擴大至 2.04 億元。由於大
客戶平板電腦、筆記型電腦相關需求受到記憶體缺料影響而降
溫，03~05/2026 營收逐月減少， 05/2026 更僅 31.98 億元， MoM-
17.86% ，為近幾年最低，估計 2Q26 營收 QoQ-22.13% 。  
預估 2026 年營收 YoY-7.88% ：展望 2026 年，預估 GIS-KY 以
車載產品營收成長性較佳，原因在於歷經多年耕耘後，客戶家
數、承接專案數量均持續增加，預估營收比重將由 2025 年的
3~4% 提高至 2026 年 5%以上。不過，預估 2026 年 GIS-KY 營收
YoY-7.88% ，原因有二， (1) 筆記型電腦、平板電腦等消費性電
子產品需求 2H26 仍持續受到記憶體缺料影響， (2)Sharp 將於
4Q26 關閉大尺寸面板產線，預估亦將衝擊 GIS-KY 筆記型電腦
相關產品的出貨表現，且預估 2027 年影響程度將再擴大。  
光通訊為目前重要股價題材： 在 GIS-KY 轉型方向中， SRG 
waveguide 模組開發進度最快， 1H26 已小量出貨，惟單月訂單
數量僅數萬片，預估 2026 年營收貢獻仍有限，群益認為，出貨
潛力最佳者仍首推與最大客戶 AR glass 相關的 Micro LED 技
術、 SRG waveguide 模組，預估相關訂單最快於 2H27~1H28 釋
出。  
此外， GIS-KY 亦切入光通訊領域，發展重心在光源 (主要為 CW 
laser) 的封裝 (die bond) 、測試， GIS-KY 2026 年預計斥資 13 億
元購置相關設備，預計 1H27 量產，為 GIS-KY 目前重要股價題
材。

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就因應氣候變遷或溫室氣體管理而言 ， GIS-KY 主要策略有三 ，(1) 建立 TCFD
風險評估小組、溫室氣體管理專職部門， (2) 設定 SBTi 科學碳目標，在中長
期目標時間內推動減碳， (3) 推動可再生能源使用。至於 GIS-KY 溫室氣體排
放量減量目標則主要有三， (1) 溫室氣體排放範疇一、三之絕對量在 2030 年
以前減少 42%，範疇二則實現净零排放， (2) 價值鏈凈零排放在 2050 年以前
實現， (3) 中國廠區範疇二在 2023 年以前實現凈零排放。此外， GIS-KY 在
中國廠區綠電使用比重達 100%， 達成大客戶減少供應鏈碳排放的需求 ， GIS-
KY 並設定極具商業野心的 1.5 度科學減碳目標，展現公司對全球氣候治理
的責任。此外，  
再就能源管理而言， GIS-KY 主要動作有二， (1) 各廠區分別提出節能專案、
專項節能計畫，並向集團申報，提升能源使用效率，以及最大優化能耗結構，
(2) 除鼓勵各部門與團隊使用再生物料，且設定內部再生物料使用目標外，
GIS-KY 並建立監測評估機制，以追蹤再生物料使用與可持續發展方面的進
展。  
消費性電子景氣受記憶體缺料影響，估計 2Q26 營收 QoQ-22.13% ：  
GIS-KY 為觸控面板廠，產品終端應用包括平板電腦、筆記型電腦等，近幾
年營收比重分別為 50~70% 、20~40% ，訂單多數來自最大客戶，目前該客戶
營收比重超過 80%，尤其，該客戶平板電腦銷售狀況更為 GIS-KY 營收表現
的重要觀察指標。然而， GIS-KY 筆記型電腦 LCM 係配合同屬鴻海 (2317 TT)
集團的面板廠 Sharp 向大客戶爭取訂單，惟 Sharp 決定 4Q26 關閉大尺寸面
板生產線，此舉對 GIS-KY 筆記型電腦 LCM 出貨表現衝擊甚鉅。  
此外，手機相關產品近幾年營收比重為 5~15% ，營收多數來自指紋辨識模
組，產品包括超音波指紋辨識模組 (目前主要用於螢幕下指紋辨識 )、電容式
指紋辨識模組 (近期多用於 Smartphone 、平板電腦側邊 )。  
近幾年 GIS-KY 積極轉型，現階段主軸為 「以半導體製程生產光學零組件」 ，
重要方向包括 Micro LED 技術、 SRG (Surface Relief Grating ，表面光柵 ) 
waveguide ( 光波導 )模組、AR HUD( 抬頭顯示器 ， 主要供車用)、optical lens( 光
學鏡片，如 metalens 、 pancake lens 、 casting lens 等 )，尤其， Micro LED 技
術、 SRG waveguide 模組均與大客戶開發 AR glass 相關，潛在商機想像空間
大，且為 GIS-KY 重要股價題材。  
1Q26 營收 160.78 億元， QoQ-2.06% ，與 4Q25 相去有限，不過，毛利率受
到產品結構 (高毛利率機種出貨比重降低 )影響而降低至 4.98%(4Q25 為
5.73%) ，本業虧損擴大至 3.21 億元 (4Q25 本業虧損 2.69 億元 )，稅後虧損擴
大至 2.04 億元 (4Q25 稅後虧損 1.37 億元 )。  
由於大客戶平板電腦、筆記型電腦相關需求受到記憶體缺料影響而降溫，
03~05/2026 營收逐月減少， 05/2026 更僅 31.98 億元， MoM-17.86% ，為近幾
年最低，估計 2Q26 營收 QoQ-22.13% 。

2H26 訂單能見度偏低，預估 2026 年營收 YoY-7.88% ：

3 群益投顧 
展望 2026 年，預估 GIS-KY 以車載產品營收成長性較佳，原因在於歷經多
年耕耘後，客戶家數、承接專案數量均持續增加，預估營收比重將由 2025
年的 3~4% 提高至 2026 年 5%以上。不過，預估 2026 年 GIS-KY 營收 YoY-
7.88% ，原因有二， (1) 筆記型電腦、平板電腦等消費性電子產品需求 2H26
仍持續受到記憶體缺料影響， (2)Sharp 將於 4Q26 關閉大尺寸面板產線，預
估亦將衝擊 GIS-KY 筆記型電腦相關產品的出貨表現，且預估 2027 年影響
程度將再擴大。  
切入光通訊領域為目前重要股價題材：  
在 GIS-KY 轉型方向中， SRG waveguide 模組開發進度最快， 1H26 已小量
出貨，惟單月訂單數量僅數萬片，預估 2026 年營收貢獻仍有限，群益認為，
出貨潛力最佳者仍首推與最大客戶 AR glass 相關的 Micro LED 技術、 SRG 
waveguide 模組，預估相關訂單最快於 2H27~1H28 釋出。  
此外， GIS-KY 亦切入光通訊領域，發展重心在光源 (主要為 CW laser) 的封
裝 (die bond) 、測試， GIS-KY 2026 年預計斥資 13 億元購置相關設備，預計
1H27 量產，為 GIS-KY 目前重要股價題材。  
雖 2026 年本業展望欠佳，但考量 AR glass 、光通訊題材，群益對 GIS-KY
的投資建議為 Trading Buy ：  
雖然記憶體缺料影響消費性電子景氣、 Sharp 4Q26 關閉大尺寸面板產線均
不利於 GIS-KY 2026 年本業表現，不過， GIS-KY 與最大客戶共同開發 AR 
glass 、切入光通訊領域均為重要股價題材，故群益對 GIS-KY 的投資建議為
Trading Buy 。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

6805 富世達(FOSITEK)

壹、投 資 建 議

一、伺服器佔比突破五成， 1Q26 淡季不淡  
富世達 1Q26 營收 41.8 億， QoQ +5.2% ，YoY +85.8% ，受惠於
伺服器產品強勁需求， 1Q26 呈現淡季不淡。產品組合來看，
1Q26 伺服器產品 (包含快接頭、滑軌 )佔整體營收的 56.4% ；折疊
手機佔 37.7% ；筆記型電腦佔 5.2% 。獲利方面， 1Q26 營業毛利
13.5 億， QoQ +17.1% ，YoY +171.2% ，毛利率 32.3% ，QoQ 
+3.2 ppts ，YoY +10.2 ppts ，受惠於伺服器產品比重提升，以及
產線擴充、自動化導入，帶動毛利率提升。營業淨利 11.2 億，
QoQ +28.9% ，YoY +206.3% ，營利率 26.8% ，QoQ +4.9 ppts ，
YoY +10.5 ppts ，1Q26 業外淨利 0.45 億，稅後淨利 9.17 億，
QoQ +22.1% ，YoY +157.3% ，單季稅後 EPS 13.38 元。

二、手機逢產品轉換期，伺服器動能續強，估 2Q26 營
運持穩向上  
公司公告 5 月營收 11.8 億， MoM -12.8% ，YoY +37.2% ，今年累
計營收 67.17 億， YoY +69.5% 。展望 2Q26 ，公司預估 2Q26 營
收持平至季增 5% ，主要反映手機軸承專案進入轉換期，預估
2Q26 手機部分營收將放緩。而伺服器產品部分，隨著 GB300 機
櫃產品進入出貨高峰， VR 機櫃產品於 2Q26 季底開始小量出貨，
預期 2Q26 伺服器產品營收將延續強勁出貨動能。另外，毛利方
面，則受惠於伺服器產品比重提升，預估毛利率將略微提升。預
估 2Q26 營收 42.6 億元， QoQ +1.8% ，YoY +62.5% ，毛利率
33.0% ，營利率 27.6% ，稅後淨利 9.77 億，單季稅後 EPS 14.26
元。

三、預估 2026/2027 稅後 EPS 63.76/94.64 元 
展望 2026 年，公司 2H26 營收將明顯優於上半年，並預估第三季
與第四季的營收將達到雙位數季增。受惠於 VR、LPU 及 ASIC 專
案的強勁需求，公司快接頭 (QD) 產品成長動能明確。隨著 VR 機
櫃自  2H26 起逐步放量，公司的產品應用將由 Switch Tray 延伸
至 Compute Tray (VR 機櫃快接頭總用量為 450 顆，相較於
GB300 機櫃 360 顆)。此外， LPU 機櫃 QD 亦預計將於 4Q26 開
始出貨，而 ASIC 部分， AWS 平台相關產品亦預計自 3Q26 起陸
續量產。受惠於新專案的陸續發酵，將顯著推升公司伺服器產品
的營收貢獻。滑軌部分，目前滑軌營收貢獻仍以通用型伺服器產
投資評等：買進

預估 2027 全年營收 268.5 億元， YoY +40.0% ，毛利率 34.9% ，
營利率 29.0% ，稅後淨利 64.9 億，全年稅後 EPS 94.64 元。

四、投資建議為「買進」，目標價 2460 元 
考量到伺服器水冷滲透率持續提升， VR、LPU 及 ASIC 專案的強
勁需求，公司快接頭 (QD) 產品成長動能明確，公司受惠於新專案
的陸續發酵，將顯著推升公司伺服器產品的營收貢獻。而滑軌部
分，公司亦持續推進 AI 伺服器新案的認證與導入，未來將有望持
續挹注營收。此外，折疊手機方面，隨著下半年主要客戶新機推
升，亦有望帶動公司營收與獲利維持強勁增長。以公司 2027 年
EPS 來看，目前股價交易於 2027 年 EPS 約 19~20 倍本益比，評
價仍具上修空間，給於買進投資建議，目標價 2460 元(26x 2027 
EPS) 。

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
富世達 (FOSITEK) 成立於 2001 年，為奇鋐科技 (3017) 旗下子公司，
主要從事電子類、電腦、資訊通信產業等 3C 電子產品轉軸組件
之研發、製造及銷售。公司產品主要應用於電腦、電子、光學、
家電、資訊、通信、醫療設備等產品之連結軸，如商用 NB 轉軸、
工業用 NB 轉軸、軍事用 NB 轉軸、 LCD Monitor 轉軸、 PDA 轉
軸、 LCD-TV 轉軸、手機轉軸、螢幕懸臂支架、醫療懸臂支架等。
公司初期與京東方、華為三方合作生產研發摺疊手機，為華為摺
疊手機的主要開案合作廠商。近年積極投入水冷快接頭模組量產，
並也陸續送給鴻海、廣達等組裝廠進行測試，同時也通過數家水
冷板供應商認證，已成功打入輝達 GB200 與 GB300 供應鏈出貨
水冷快接頭 (QD/UQD) 與滑軌， 2025 年折疊手機佔公司整體營收
的 56.4% ，筆記型電腦佔 6.4% ，伺服器產品佔 36.3% ，其他佔
0.9% 。公司總部位於台北新莊新北產業園區，並於大陸深圳及越
南設有生產據點。公司 AI 伺服器與水冷零件主要客戶為 NVIDIA
及 CSP 大廠；手機主要客戶包含華為、 Motorola ，Google 等；

二. 營運概況及展望

圖一、富世達 1Q26 產品營收佔比

資料來源：富世達

1. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 28% 
根據 TrendForce 預估，全球 AI 伺服器出貨量有望維持強勁增長，
年增率預估將突破 28% ，並同步帶動整體伺服器市場出貨量年增
12.8% 。受惠於北美五大 CSP 業者 (Google 、 AWS 、 Meta 、
Microsoft 、Oracle)2026 年資本支出總額預計大幅年增 40% ，為
AI 基礎設施提供了堅實的資金支撐。其次，需求重心由「訓練」
轉向「推論」，隨著 AI Agents 、LLaMA 模型應用及 Copilot 升
級，雲端大廠為因應龐大的推論流量與變現需求，不僅持續追加
AI 伺服器訂單，更同步帶動通用型伺服器 (General Server) 進入
新一輪的汰換與擴張週期。最後，自研 ASIC 的擴張動能，在追
求成本優化與特定任務效率的驅動下， 2026 年 ASIC AI 伺服器出
貨占比預計將攀升至 27.8% ，其出貨增速將超越 GPU 機種；儘管
GPU 仍保有近七成市佔，且以  NVIDIA GB300 系列為市場主流、
VR200 預計於下半年逐步放量，但 ASIC 出貨比重的顯著提升，
已成為 2026 年伺服器市場結構性擴張的關鍵指標。

圖二、 2023-2026 全球 Server 、AI Server 出貨量年增率預估

資料來源： TrendForce 、宏遠投顧整理

2. AI 晶片熱功耗推升需求，液冷滲透率加速提升  
隨著 GPU 與 ASIC 晶片功耗大幅提升，以 NVIDIA GB200/GB300 
NVL72 系統為例，單櫃熱設計功耗 (TDP) 高達 130-140kW ，遠超
過傳統氣冷極限，因而率先導入液對氣 (Liquid-to-Air, L2A) 冷卻
技術。受制於既有資料中心建築與水循環設施，短期內 L2A 成為
主流過渡方案，但隨著新一代資料中心自 2025 年起陸續完工，
再加上 AI 晶片功耗與系統密度持續升級，預期液對液 (Liquid-to-
Liquid, L2L) 架構將於 2027 年起快速普及，逐步取代 L2A 成為主
流散熱模式。根據 TrendForce 預估，隨著 NVIDIA GB200 NVL72
機櫃式伺服器於 2025 年放量出貨，雲端業者加速升級 AI 資料中
心架構，促使液冷技術也進入規模化導入階段。液冷在 AI 資料中
心的滲透率將由 2024 年的 14% 大幅提升至 2025 年的 33% ，並
於 2026 年達到 40% ，推動冷卻模組、熱交換系統與週邊零組件
需求持續擴張。作為接觸式熱交換核心的冷水板 (Cold Plate ）需
求尤為顯著，主要供應商包括 Cooler Master 、AVC 、BOYD 和
Auras ，當中，除 BOYD 之外，其餘業者均已於東南亞擴建產能，
以因應美系 CSP 客戶高強度的需求。

3. 摺疊手機迎結構性擴張，預估 2026 年出貨年增 20% 
根據 Counterpoint Research 發布報告， 2026 年第一季全球摺疊
手機出貨量展現強勁韌性，交出年增  49% 的亮眼成績 (對比同期
全球整體智慧型手機市場約 6%的衰退 )；展望 2026 全年，全球
摺疊手機出貨量預估將維持約 20% 的強勢年成長，意味著摺疊手
機市場已擺脫短期的「週期性波動」，正式步入長期穩健的「結
構性增長」階段。而強勁的成長動能主要源自三大核心：首先是
「蘋果 (Apple) 的重磅入局」，市場預期蘋果將於下半年推出首款
摺疊 iPhone ，不僅將大幅提升消費者的接受度，更將重塑高階手
機的競爭版圖；其次是「書本式 (Book-Type) 設計的全面爆發」，
由於各家 Android 品牌廠為了抵抗中低階市場的價格戰並追求更
高的平均售價 (ASP) 與利潤，紛紛將資源集中於高規格的書本式
摺疊機，預估該型態將拿下 2026 年全年 65% 的摺疊機市佔率；
最後則是「軟體體驗與生產力的躍進」，消費者的購買決策已從
早期單純追求鉸鏈硬體或外觀新奇，轉向無縫的應用程式接續
(App Continuity) 與多視窗多工處理能力，這些實用價值的明確化，
成功驅動了摺疊手機從實驗性的利基產品，蛻變為高階市場的核
心成長引擎。

圖四、摺疊手機出貨量及書本式摺疊手機佔比預估

資料來源： Counterpoint

持續挹注營收。另外，手機軸承部分，主要客戶預計 2H26 陸續
推出新款摺疊手機及新款三折疊機，其中，公司作為三折機軸承
的獨家供應商，預計下半年整體手機軸承出貨量動能將顯著優於
上半年。預估 2026 全年營收 191.8 億元， YoY +54.5% ，毛利率
33.2% ，營利率 27.4% ，稅後淨利 43.7 億，全年稅後 EPS 63.76
元。展望 2027 年，受惠於伺服器液冷滲透率持續提升， AI 機櫃
產品延續強勁成長動能，將有望公司帶動營收、獲利持續走高。
預估 2027 全年營收 268.5 億元， YoY +40.0% ，毛利率 34.9% ，
營利率 29.0% ，稅後淨利 64.9 億，全年稅後 EPS 94.64 元。

### Page 2

2

國內有害廢棄物處理大廠 ：可寧衛主要從事有害廢棄物的清除處理相關業務，業務
包括有害廢棄物固化與開挖、廢棄物掩埋和清運，近幾年積極拓展其他業務，如有
害廢棄物廠可威環境(前身為可寧衛蘇伊士)、紙類回收(上評資源)、廢棄照明回收
的中台資源(6923)和漁電共生的大創綠能 。2025 年固化佔 22%， 開挖營收比重15
％(高雄煉油廠整治工程挹注)，掩埋佔營收比重 33％、清運佔 3％，製紙佔 9％，
售電收入佔 13%。獲利率掩埋優於固化優於清運，由於掩埋場取得困難，因此掩
埋場可使用的期限與空間是公司最大的資產。 
高雄 205 兵工廠污染場址案挹注固化營收：高雄兵工廠 205 廠污染整治計畫總經
費為新台幣 37.46 億元，分階段進行，第一期工程施行到2025/8 月，因開挖後有
害廢棄物較多增加固化營收提升毛利率，2、3 區第一季已點，目前開挖中，預計
工程到 1Q27。 
高雄南區焚化爐 BOT 案：可寧衛與台灣人壽成立吉信公司，持股 55%，共同投資
150 億元，在建新的焚化爐時同時操作舊的焚化爐，新廠廢棄物 42 萬噸/年
(24%YoY)，售電度數 726.5 度/噸，售電單價 3.9482 元，售電收入 12.05 億元，
待完工後可貢獻 18.8 億元，由於舊爐歲修頻率高導致稼動率較低，1Q26 認列投
資損失 1533 萬元，公司希望 4Q 有望翻正，另外 5 月起每月需認列建置焚化爐的
營收但同步認列成本，故無毛利貢獻。 
可威虧損持續：榮工處所屬的大發焚化爐民營化案，焚化處理 2.92 萬噸/年，物化
5 萬噸/年，台灣第一家物化處理廠，可寧衛、台灣威立雅資源(原名台灣蘇伊士新
創建環境服務)與榮工工程於 2018/7/31 共同設立可威環境資源股分有限公司，
2018/11/1 取得大發廠業務，可寧衛持有 29%股權，一加二期產能為 130 噸/日，
虧損多年後，2025 年一度翻正，惟 1Q26 非預期歲修增加認列再度轉為虧損，認
列投資損失 2053 萬元(虧損擴大)，2Q 預計虧損縮小，力拼下半年轉正。 
中台資源為轉投資收益主要貢獻：中台公司(6923) 汞廢棄物處理及廢照明光源資
源回收再利用事業及綠色產業事業，資本額 9.19 億元，焚化 3700 噸/月，固化
2000 噸/月，化學 2400 噸/月，目前申請二期擴建，預計再投入 3 億元增加固液
混燒設備 ， 預計2027 年試營運 。 可寧衛持有18.62%，2025 年認列投資收益 0.43
億元(-45%YoY)，1Q26 年認列 0.12 億元(-13%YoY)。  
大承再生料粒廠預計 2H26 商轉：大承再生粒料廠使用固化和熱處理無機或有機
污泥等製成再生粒料，交給吉鼎作成低強度回填材料 (CLSM)，大承循環再生粒料

廠預計 2H26 正式商轉，月處理量為9,000 噸，屆時吉鼎混凝土營收可望成長。大

可寧衛能源的觀音再生燃料廠預計 2Q27 完工：可寧衛持有可寧衛能源 75%，可

售出，合約簽 20 年，總投資 33 億元，地點在觀音鄉，雖然 SRF 有爭議，但透過
設備採購排放標準提升，公司預期 2Q27 仍可完工運轉，2025 年可寧衛認列可寧
衛能源投資損失 0.59 億元，1Q26 認列虧損 0.28 億元。

### Page 4

資料中心三層互連架構
3
10號字
8號字
7號字
註1：銀行準備金是指商業銀行
存放在聯邦儲備銀行（Fed）
帳戶中的資金
註2：隔夜逆回購協議 (ON
RRP)，是聯準會執行的一種公
開市場操作工具，用來從市場
上吸收多餘的流動性
資料來源與期間 :  Bloomberg，1980/1~2026/6。
AI基礎設施正由單機櫃運算邁向跨機櫃集群運算 。此一演進讓更多 GPU維持在頻寬最高、延遲最
低同一層網路內協調運作 ，大幅提升對高速網路互連與訊號完整性的需求 ，並顯著推升系統單位
價值。依傳輸距離，互聯架構可以分為三層： Scale-up(機櫃內)將多顆GPU連結為單一運算節點
，要求極高頻寬與極低延遲 、Scale-out(跨機櫃)聚焦機櫃間互聯，需要求高頻寬與低延遲 。光互
聯為主流方案，以400G/800G/1.6T光模組連接。隨集群擴大、功耗與成本急升CPO(共封裝光學
)成為焦點、Scale-across(跨資料中心)將分散於不同區域的資料中心互聯為更大規模的AI工廠。

### Page 5

速率、距離、規模、功耗逼近極限，光通訊成為關鍵技術
4
10號字
8號字
7號字
註1：銀行準備金是指商業銀行
存放在聯邦儲備銀行（Fed）
帳戶中的資金
註2：隔夜逆回購協議 (ON
RRP)，是聯準會執行的一種公
開市場操作工具，用來從市場
上吸收多餘的流動性
AI基礎設施正由單一機櫃運算 ，演進至跨機櫃的大規模集群運算 。此一趨勢使更多GPU需在高頻
寬、低延遲的同一網路層級協同運作 ，大幅推升對高速互連與訊號完整性的要求 。在此架構下，
「以距離決定傳輸介質 」成為關鍵：短距離 （機櫃內）：銅纜（DAC）成本最低、效率最佳、中
距離（跨機櫃）：光收發模組成為主流。隨著傳輸速率升級至 1.6T / 3.2T，可插拔光模組在功耗
與訊號完整性面臨瓶頸 ，光學進一步向晶片靠近 ，推動架構升級至CPO（共同封裝光學），以支
撐更高頻寬與更低功耗需求。

### Page 10

在GB300世代中 ，NVL72架構以單一機櫃搭載 72顆GPU作為一個計算單元 ，而在下一代 Rubin
Ultra平台中，NVL576則進一步提升至8個機櫃、共576顆GPU的超大規模架構。此一演進代表AI
基礎設施由單機櫃運算邁向跨機櫃集群運算 ，大幅提升對高速互連與訊號傳輸的需求 ，並顯著推
升整體系統的單位價值 ，進而帶動光模組 、CPO光引擎、高速銅纜及高階 PCB中介板等關鍵領域
需求同步成長。

### Page 11

隨著 AI 資料中心連接速率由 112G 持續升級至 224G 甚至 448G，銅纜技術亦由早期的 DAC(被
動銅纜)，逐步演進至 ACC(半主動銅纜)，再進一步升級至 AEC(主動電纜)。此一演進主要反映在
高速傳輸環境下 ，訊號衰減與通道損耗日益嚴重 ，單純依賴被動銅纜已難以維持訊號完整性 ，因
此需透過 redriver(ACC) 或 retimer(AEC) 來進行訊號重整與補償，以延長可傳輸距離並提升穩定
性。儘管整體連接技術正朝光通訊發展 ，銅纜仍憑藉成本低 、功耗小與短距離需求龐大等優勢持
續受惠，並透過技術升級延長應用週期，在AI資料中心中維持關鍵角色。
銅纜互連技術演進從被動銅纜到主動銅纜

### Page 12

隨著 AI 算力需求快速提升，資料中心內部互連架構亦由機櫃內延伸至機櫃間，帶動 PCB 應用由
單純板上走線（PCB traces）進一步升級至中介層板（PCB midplane）。在短距離 (<1m) 傳輸
場景中，PCB traces 具備成本最低優勢，主要用於機櫃內 GPU 與 GPU 之間連接，整體而言，AI
伺服器連接正朝向高頻高速與跨機櫃擴展發展，推升 PCB、CCL 及高速互連材料需求同步升級。

銅纜互連技術演進DAC(被動銅纜)→ACC(半主動銅纜)→AEC(主動銅纜)

### Page 13

隨著 AI 資料中心對高頻寬與大規模連接需求提升，連接技術呈現由電走向光的演進趨勢。短距離
仍以 PCB 與銅纜為主，如 PCB traces、DAC、ACC 與 AEC，具備低成本與低功耗優勢；隨距離
與速率提升，訊號衰減加劇，光通訊逐漸成為主流，應用自 AOC、DR 延伸至 FR。另一方面，光
技術亦由過去主要用於  scale-out，開始導入 scale-up架構，並從可插拔光模組進一步發展至
NPO 與 CPO，以支撐更高密度與更低延遲的AI運算需求。
光通訊應用範圍持續擴展

隨著 AI 訓練與推論需求快速成長，資料中心網路架構正由傳統銅互連逐步走向高速光互連，帶動
台灣相關供應鏈全面升級 。在上游部分，聯亞與全新提供雷射與磊晶等關鍵光源材料 ，為光模組
與矽光技術奠定基礎；中游則由上詮、萬潤等切入 CPO 封裝與光纖耦合，以及欣興、臻鼎與台光

電供應高速 PCB 與高階 CCL 材料，支撐高頻高速訊號傳輸需求；銅纜方面則由貿聯-KY 提供短距
離高速連接解決方案。整體而言，在 AI 網路頻寬由 800G 持續升級至 1.6T 甚至 3.2T 的趨勢下，
台廠憑藉在光、電與材料製程上的優勢，將同時受惠於光模組、CPO 與高速互連需求成長，推動
AI 資料中心連接技術全面升級。

Inverter/UPS 復甦仍未明朗，估 2Q26 營收年減 5%  
展望 2026 年，管理層考量總體經濟、關稅及地緣政治不確定性仍高，因此
將 2026全年營收展望調整為年減 0-5%。考量 1)中東戰爭影響；2) 低功率
Inverter中國競爭壓力；3) UPS 價格調漲使終端需求尚未明朗，下調 2Q26
營收 15.3%至 54.3億元，季增 29.0%；下調毛利率 3.2個百分點至 27.1%，
季增 1.0ppts，主因 Inverter產品毛利率下降；業外損失估 1.2億元，主要考
量人民幣升值影響，EPS估 8.36元，季增 87.1%/年減 51.1%，下修 36.3%。 
PSU+BBU 產品 2H26 將送樣測試，預計 2H27 有望貢獻 
隨 NVIDIA Rubin Ultra 新一代 AI平台功耗持續提升，電力架構正朝向 800V
及± 400V HVDC 發展。旭隼 與 Schneider 合作，已完成 18.5kW 800V 
HVDC PSU 及 BBU產品開發，預計近月將完成 Prototype送給客戶認證，
2H26 進入系統整合測試。公司目前產品電流轉換效率已達 97.6%以上並通
過 OCP 負載測試，估最快 2H27 開始貢獻營收，推估 800 HVDC 
PSU+BBU單櫃代工金額為 20萬美金，以 3萬櫃計算，2027年整體市場規
模為 60億美金，2028年隨滲透率翻倍成長至 120億美金。以旭隼 1%市佔
率推估，預估 800 HVDC 產品 2027/2028年有望分別貢獻 20/50億元營收，
佔旭隼 2027年營收比重達 9%。 
800V HVDC 市場商機具想像力，給予目標價 1,100 元 
此次下修 2026年 EPS 33%，主要考量客戶拉貨保守以及人民幣升值因素。
我們認為旭隼股價已反應 2026 年下修預期， 1Q26 為全年營收低點，隨
800V HVDC 產品 2H26 將送樣測試，預計 2H27 有望貢獻，預估
2026/2027年 EPS分別為 30.79元/40.45元，長線而言，除 Schneider外，
Vertiv及 Eaton 等業者皆為公司長期合作客戶，旭隼有望透過既有客戶擴大
滲透率，題材具想像空間，因此維持買進，目標價 1,100 元，係根據 27x 
2027預估 EPS 40.45元推得。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
展望 2026 年，管理層考量總體經濟、關稅及地緣政治不確定性仍高，
因此將 2026 全年營收展望調整為年減 0-5%。 
⧫  
旭隼 800V HVDC PSU+BBU 產品 2H26 將送樣測試，預估 2027/2028
年分別貢獻 20/50 億元營收，佔 2027 年營收比重達 9%。 
⧫  
我們認為旭隼股價已反應 2026 年下修預期，1Q26 為全年營收低點，
維持買進，目標價 1,100 元，係根據 27x 2027F EPS 40.45 元推得。

### Page 2

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電池與電池零組件 2026年 6月 25日 Voltronic 第2 頁，共 16頁
營運分析
中東戰爭、中國競爭壓力以及匯率影響，1Q26 EPS 低於市場預期 51%
旭隼 1Q26營收年減 15.8%至 42.1億元，主因 1) Inverter受中東戰爭影響，海運不通導致出貨受
阻；2)對等關稅客戶調整終端價格，使需求呈現觀望；毛利率季減 2.9個百分點/年減 3.5個百分點
至 26.1%，低於市場預期 2.6 個百分點，主因金屬原物料大幅上漲影響，使營業利益季減 21.3%/
年減 28.6%至 7.6億元，低於市場預期 19.5%；業外損失 2.63億元，主因人民幣兌台幣升值，公
司應付帳款以人民幣為主，匯兌損失 3-4億元；EPS 4.46元，季減 26.6%/年減 54.1%，低於市場
預期 51.4%。
三大事業群 1Q26狀況如下：
➢ On-line UPS：營收占比 41%，營收年減 10%，主因 1)UPS終端價格調漲，需求採觀望；2)

原物料銀價格上漲，上游有惜售狀況。
➢ Off-line UPS：營收占比 30%，營收年減 10%，主因 UPS終端價格調漲，需求採觀望。
➢ Inverter：營收占比 19%，營收年減 38%，毛利率低於 On-line UPS，主因持續面臨中國新
進者壓力，導致毛利率下滑，其中低功率 inverter影響最大，因此公司進行策略性調整，毛利
率<20%就會放棄相關產品發展，將朝向高毛利率商用 inverter發展。巴基斯坦 Inverter市場
需求有回升，然而整體中東市場仍受戰事影響，客戶態度偏觀望；南非市場需求則尚未回溫。

Inverter/UPS 復甦仍未明朗，估 2Q26/3Q26 營收分別年減 5%/年增 6% 
考量 1)中東戰爭影響持續；2) 低功率 Inverter持續受到中國競爭；3) UPS價格調漲使終端需求回
溫狀況尚未明朗，本中心下調 2Q26營收 15.3%至 54.3億元，季增 29.0%/年減 4.9%；下調毛利

元，季增 11.4%/年增 27.2%，下修 21.2%。
